    ["Clarice Jensen", "Grey McMurray", "", "Jensen and Mcmurray have played on three of the same records. Her cello is on his album Crying at Breakfast."],
    ["Mary Lattimore", "William Tyler", "", "Tyler and Lattimore share three records, among them H.C. McEntire's Lionheart and North Americans' Roped In."],
    ["Clarice Jensen", "Julianna Barwick", "", "Both play on Sharon Van Etten's Tramp."],
    ["Clarice Jensen", "Cowboy Sadness", "", "Jensen's cello and Peter Silberman of Cowboy Sadness both appear on two Son Lux albums, We Are Rising and Lanterns."],
    ["Cowboy Sadness", "Dave Harrington", "", "Harrington remixed The Antlers and plays on their album Green to Gold, which features all three members of Cowboy Sadness."],
    ["Mary Lattimore", "Walt McClements", "", "Lattimore and McClements made the duo album Rain on the Road in 2024, and she plays on his production for Lonesome Leash."],
    ["Balmorhea", "Clarice Jensen", "", "Jensen played cello on Balmorhea's The Wind and Pendant World, both released by Deutsche Grammophon."],
    ["Balmorhea", "Rachel Grimes", "", "Grimes and Balmorhea's Travis Chapman both play on Rutger Zuydervelt's Stay Tuned."],
    ["Alan Sparhawk", "Pan American", "", "Sparhawk plays on Pan American's 360 Business / 360 Bypass, released in 1999."],
    ["Alan Sparhawk", "Rachel Grimes", "", "Both played on Shannon Wright's Perishable Goods in 2001."],
    ["Cowboy Sadness", "Stephanie Coleman", "", "David Moore of Cowboy Sadness and Coleman recorded 'Diamond Shoals' together, and both play on Jumping Through Hoops' Rockin' to the Fiddle."],
    ["Kyle Hamlett Duo", "William Tyler", "", "Both play on Chelsea Crowell's self-titled album."],
    ["Marisa Anderson", "Mary Lattimore", "", "Both play on Sharon Van Etten's Are We There."],
    ["East Forest", "Hammock", "", "Hammock remixed East Forest for East Forest x Ram Dass: Reworks in 2019."],
    ["Balmorhea", "Cowboy Sadness", "", "David Moore of Cowboy Sadness reworked a track for Lower River Reworks, the solo release by Balmorhea's Michael A. Muller."],
    ["Marisa Anderson", "William Tyler", "", "Anderson and Tyler made the duo album Lost Futures in 2021."],
    ["Chuck Johnson", "claire rousay", "", "Both play on Arian Shafiee's Pastorale."],
    ["Chuck Johnson", "Clarice Jensen", "", "Jensen played cello on Johnson's album Burden of Proof."],
    ["Chuck Johnson", "marine eyes", "", "Both appear on Kenneth James Gibson's Further Translations, where Johnson also contributed a remix."],
    ["Marisa Anderson", "Walt McClements", "", "Both play on Tara Jane O'Neil's The Cool Cloud of Okayness."],
    ["Dave Harrington", "Grey McMurray", "", "Both play on Cassandra Jenkins' My Light, My Destroyer."],
    ["Nate Mercereau", "Nicole McCabe", "", "Both play on Julius Rodriguez's Evergreen."],
    ["Nailah Hunter", "Yara Asmar", "", "Both appear on Louis Carnell's album 111, released by Mute."],
    ["Julianna Barwick", "Meg Baird", "", "Both appear on aus's album Fluctor."],
    ["Cowboy Sadness", "Mary Lattimore", "", "Lattimore and David Moore of Cowboy Sadness both play on Ezra Feinberg's Soft Power."],
    ["Chelsea Bridge", "Pan American", "", "Chelsea Bridge plays on Pan American's Fly the Ocean in a Silver Plane, and the two are co-credited on 'New News'."],
    ["Clarice Jensen", "MJ Guider", "", "Both contributed remixes to Benoit Pioulard's Stanza IV."],
    ["Brad Barr", "Nicole McCabe", "", "Both play on Karneef's It's How You Say It."],
    ["Lake Mary", "Stelth Ulvang", "", "Ulvang plays on Lake Mary's Neutral Crafting of Life."],
    ["Chuck Johnson", "Walt McClements", "", "Johnson mixed and plays on McClements' album On a Painted Ocean."],
    ["Julianna Barwick", "William Tyler", "", "Barwick and Tyler recorded 'Drift of the Jenny' together for Northern Spy."],
    ["Chuck Johnson", "Cole Pulice", "", "Johnson mixed and plays on Pulice's album Land's End Eternal."],
  ];

  /* ══ DC-DEFAULTS:END run=13 keep=CONNECTIONS ══ */
  // THE PHONE READS THIS TABLE THROUGH PH_CONNS. Collapsed 2026-08-31: these
  // were two byte-identical 53-row arrays. The split existed for shorter phone
  // copy, and STORY_H = 54 removed that need by growing the box to fit this
  // corpus at phone type size — so the second copy had no function except to
  // be identical, which is a divergence waiting to happen.
  //
  // phoneMap still reads PH_CONNS, so the phone read-path is unchanged and the
  // downstream divergence test now passes structurally rather than by
  // coincidence. If phone-specific copy is ever genuinely needed, add a
  // per-row override here — never a second full corpus.
  PH_CONNS = this.data('CONNECTIONS');

  LINK_KEY = 'ath-curator-links';

  loadLinks() {
    try { const v = window.localStorage.getItem(this.LINK_KEY); return v ? JSON.parse(v) : []; }
    catch (e) { return []; }
  }

  saveLinks(links) {
    try { window.localStorage.setItem(this.LINK_KEY, JSON.stringify(links)); } catch (e) {}
  }

  pickLink(name) {
    const from = this.state.linkFrom;
    if (!from) { this.setState({ linkFrom: name }); return; }
    if (from === name) { this.setState({ linkFrom: null }); return; }
    const links = (this.state.links || []).concat([[from, name]]);
    this.saveLinks(links);
    this.setState({ links: links, linkFrom: null });
  }

  removeLink(i) {
    const links = (this.state.links || []).filter((x, k) => k !== i);
    this.saveLinks(links);
    this.setState({ links: links });
  }

  buildFree(kind, W, st) {
    // A phone has no 24px chrome gutter here — the card already insets — so the
    // field claims the full width rather than the desktop's inset.
    const avail = (this.VPS[st.vp] && this.VPS[st.vp][0] < 700) ? (W - 20) : (W - 48);
    const drift = kind === 'drift';
    const cell = avail / 8;
    // Portrait: two volume COLUMNS, eight drops running down each.
    const phone = (this.VPS[st.vp] && this.VPS[st.vp][0] < 700);
    const PH_COLS = 4, PH_ROW = 128, PH_MAP_TOP = 48; // renamed 2026-08-31: was PH_TOP, which collided with the header PH_TOP = 14 declared at ~4848
    // ── Fill the height ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    // The map is WIDTH-bound: at 1132 the eight drops already span the canvas,
    // so a uniform scale cannot use the height left under the title. The
    // composition itself grows instead — faces and vertical spacing spread to
    // fill the room between the title block and the transport.
    const vp = this.VPS[st.vp] || this.VPS[0];
    const kApprox = Math.max(1, (vp[0] - 48) / 1132);
    const roomH = (vp[1] - 240) / kApprox;
    const VZ = phone ? 0.98 : drift ? Math.max(1, Math.min(1.85, roomH / 320)) : 1;
    const C = Math.round((drift ? 44 : 46) * VZ), I = Math.round(30 * VZ);
    // Sized so BOTH bands plus the blob field fit a 1180 window: the two
    // volumes are the litmus test, so the band budget comes first and the fan
    // is drawn inside it.
    const BASE = Math.round((drift ? 66 : 104) * VZ), WAVE = Math.round((drift ? 13 : 0) * VZ);
    const A_DY = Math.round((drift ? 50 : -62) * VZ), B_DY = Math.round((drift ? 96 : 62) * VZ);
    const H = Math.round((drift ? 196 : 206) * VZ);
    // ── One coordinate space for BOTH volumes ────────────────────────────
    // Cross-volume connections need a single field: every node and blocker
    // lives at a global y (volume index × BAND_OFF), the router runs once over
    // all of it, and strands are emitted into the first band, free to travel
    // down through the gap between the volumes.
    const BAND_OFF = phone ? (PH_MAP_TOP + 2 * PH_ROW) : H + 62;
    const TOT = BAND_OFF + H;

    const gBlockers = [], gNode = {}, gByName = {};
    const gFold = (n) => this.slug(String(n).split(':')[0]);
    const gPut = (id, name, node) => { gNode[id] = node; (gByName[gFold(name)] = gByName[gFold(name)] || []).push(id); };
    const volPos = [];
    this.data('VOLS').forEach((v, vi) => {
      const colX = phone ? Math.round(avail * (vi === 0 ? 0.26 : 0.74)) : 0;
      const row = [];
      v.drops.forEach((d, i) => {
        if (phone) {
          const cw = avail / 4;
          row.push({
            x: Math.round((i % 4) * cw + cw / 2),
            y: Math.round(PH_MAP_TOP + Math.floor(i / 4) * PH_ROW)
          });
        } else {
          row.push({ x: Math.round(i * cell + cell / 2), y: (drift ? Math.round(BASE + Math.sin(i * 0.85 + vi * 0.6) * WAVE) : BASE) });
        }
      });
      volPos.push(row);
      v.drops.forEach((d, i) => {
        const c = row[i], oy = vi * BAND_OFF;
        const flip = !drift && i % 2 === 1;
        const gp = phone
          ? [{ x: c.x + 30, y: c.y - 17 }, { x: c.x + 34, y: c.y + 19 }]
          : [
              { x: c.x + (drift ? -30 : (flip ? 34 : -34)), y: c.y + (drift ? A_DY : (flip ? B_DY : A_DY)) },
              { x: c.x + (drift ? 34 : (flip ? -34 : 34)), y: c.y + (drift ? B_DY : (flip ? A_DY : B_DY)) }
            ];
        const cRect = { x1: c.x - C / 2 - 3, x2: c.x + C / 2 + 3, y1: oy + c.y - C / 2 - 3, y2: oy + c.y + C / 2 + 3 };
        const cNameR = drift
          ? { x1: c.x - 60, x2: c.x + 60, y1: oy + c.y - C / 2 - 46, y2: oy + c.y - C / 2 - 2 }
          : { x1: c.x - 60, x2: c.x + 60, y1: oy + c.y + C / 2 + 2, y2: oy + c.y + C / 2 + 46 };
        gBlockers.push(cRect); gBlockers.push(cNameR);
        gPut(d[1] + ':' + vi + ':' + d[0] + ':c', d[1], { x: c.x, y: oy + c.y, r: C / 2, rect: cRect, nameRect: cNameR });
        gp.forEach((p, gi) => {
          const gRect = { x1: p.x - I / 2 - 3, x2: p.x + I / 2 + 3, y1: oy + p.y - I / 2 - 3, y2: oy + p.y + I / 2 + 3 };
          const gNameR = { x1: p.x - 50, x2: p.x + 50, y1: oy + p.y + I / 2 + 2, y2: oy + p.y + I / 2 + 44 };
          gBlockers.push(gRect); gBlockers.push(gNameR);
          gPut(d[3][gi] + ':' + vi + ':' + d[0] + ':a', d[3][gi], { x: p.x, y: oy + p.y, r: I / 2, rect: gRect, nameRect: gNameR });
        });
      });
    });
    const gResolve = (ref) => {
      const parts = String(ref).split(':');
      const ids = gByName[gFold(parts[0])] || [];
      if (!ids.length) return null;
      if (parts.length > 1) {
        const hit = ids.filter(id => id.split(':')[2] === parts[1] && (!parts[2] || id.slice(-1) === parts[2]))[0];
        if (hit) return gNode[hit];
      }
      const art = ids.filter(id => id.slice(-1) === 'a')[0];
      return gNode[art || ids[0]];
    };

    // ── Strand routing ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
    // Bearings and relaxation: a line leaves on the bearing toward its target,
    // sits on both rims, then relaxes out of faces, names and other strands
    // and is Chaikin-smoothed into a real arc.
    const strandWires = [], strandHits = [];
    const cacheKey = kind + ':' + W + ':' + this.data('CONNECTIONS').length;
    this._strandCache = this._strandCache || {};
    // Connections are told in the pill panel now, never as lines: the only
    // strokes on this map are the drop tethers.
    const needStrands = false;
    if (needStrands && !this._strandCache[cacheKey]) {
      const pairs = [];
      this.data('CONNECTIONS').forEach((cn, si) => {
        const F = gResolve(cn[0]), T = gResolve(cn[1]);
        if (!F || !T || (F.x === T.x && F.y === T.y)) return;
        pairs.push({ si: si, F: F, T: T, note: cn[2] || (cn[0] + ' → ' + cn[1]) });
      });
      const MIN_GAP = 16 * Math.PI / 180;
      const rimAngle = {}, byNode = {};
      pairs.forEach(p => {
        [['F', p.F, p.T], ['T', p.T, p.F]].forEach((tri) => {
          const end = tri[0], node = tri[1], other = tri[2];
          const k = node.x + ',' + node.y;
          (byNode[k] = byNode[k] || []).push({ p: p, end: end, bearing: Math.atan2(other.y - node.y, other.x - node.x) });
        });
      });
      Object.keys(byNode).forEach(k => {
        const list = byNode[k];
        list.sort((a2, b2) => a2.bearing - b2.bearing);
        const ang = list.map(e => e.bearing);
        for (let it = 0; it < 24; it++) {
          for (let i = 1; i < ang.length; i++) {
            const d = ang[i] - ang[i - 1];
            if (d < MIN_GAP) { const push = (MIN_GAP - d) / 2; ang[i - 1] -= push; ang[i] += push; }
          }
        }
        list.forEach((e, i) => { rimAngle[k + '|' + e.p.si + '|' + e.end] = ang[i]; });
      });
      gBlockers.push({ x1: 0, x2: 290, y1: H + 12, y2: H + 58 });
      gBlockers.push({ x1: avail - 360, x2: avail, y1: H + 12, y2: H + 58 });
      const routes = [], others = [];
      pairs.forEach((pr) => {
        const F = pr.F, T = pr.T;
        const aF = rimAngle[F.x + ',' + F.y + '|' + pr.si + '|F'];
        const aT = rimAngle[T.x + ',' + T.y + '|' + pr.si + '|T'];
        const P = { x: F.x + Math.cos(aF) * (F.r + 1), y: F.y + Math.sin(aF) * (F.r + 1) };
        const Q = { x: T.x + Math.cos(aT) * (T.r + 1), y: T.y + Math.sin(aT) * (T.r + 1) };
        const N = Math.max(16, Math.min(46, Math.round(Math.hypot(Q.x - P.x, Q.y - P.y) / 22)));
        const pts = [];
        for (let k = 0; k <= N; k++) { const t = k / N; pts.push({ x: P.x + (Q.x - P.x) * t, y: P.y + (Q.y - P.y) * t }); }
        pts[1] = { x: P.x + Math.cos(aF) * (F.r + 14), y: P.y + Math.sin(aF) * (F.r + 14) };
        pts[N - 1] = { x: Q.x + Math.cos(aT) * (T.r + 14), y: Q.y + Math.sin(aT) * (T.r + 14) };
        const MARGIN = 10;
        for (let it = 0; it < 110; it++) {
          for (let k2 = 1; k2 < N; k2++) {
            const p = pts[k2];
            let fx = 0, fy = 0;
            for (let bi = 0; bi < gBlockers.length; bi++) {
              const r = gBlockers[bi];
              if (p.x > r.x1 - MARGIN && p.x < r.x2 + MARGIN && p.y > r.y1 - MARGIN && p.y < r.y2 + MARGIN) {
                const cx = (r.x1 + r.x2) / 2, cy = (r.y1 + r.y2) / 2;
                const dx = p.x - cx, dy = p.y - cy, dl = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
                const need = Math.max((r.x2 - r.x1) / 2 + MARGIN, (r.y2 - r.y1) / 2 + MARGIN);
                fx += (dx / dl) * (need - dl > 0 ? 6 : 3);
                fy += (dy / dl) * (need - dl > 0 ? 6 : 3);
              }
            }
            for (let oi = 0; oi < others.length; oi++) {
              const o = others[oi];
              const dx = p.x - o.x, dy = p.y - o.y, d2 = dx * dx + dy * dy;
              if (d2 < 100 && d2 > 0.01) { const dl = Math.sqrt(d2); fx += (dx / dl) * (10 - dl) * 0.35; fy += (dy / dl) * (10 - dl) * 0.35; }
            }
            const mx = (pts[k2 - 1].x + (pts[k2 + 1] ? pts[k2 + 1].x : Q.x)) / 2;
            const my = (pts[k2 - 1].y + (pts[k2 + 1] ? pts[k2 + 1].y : Q.y)) / 2;
            fx += (mx - p.x) * 0.32; fy += (my - p.y) * 0.32;
            p.x = Math.max(3, Math.min(avail - 3, p.x + fx));
            p.y = Math.max(3, Math.min(TOT - 3, p.y + fy));
          }
          pts[0] = P; pts[N] = Q;
        }
        for (let k2 = 1; k2 < N; k2++) {
          const p = pts[k2];
          for (let bi = 0; bi < gBlockers.length; bi++) {
            const r = gBlockers[bi];
            if (p.x > r.x1 && p.x < r.x2 && p.y > r.y1 && p.y < r.y2) {
              const dl = [p.x - r.x1, r.x2 - p.x, p.y - r.y1, r.y2 - p.y];
              const mi2 = dl.indexOf(Math.min.apply(null, dl));
              if (mi2 === 0) p.x = r.x1 - 2; else if (mi2 === 1) p.x = r.x2 + 2;
              else if (mi2 === 2) p.y = r.y1 - 2; else p.y = r.y2 + 2;
            }
          }
        }
        let sm = pts;
        for (let c2 = 0; c2 < 2; c2++) {
          const nx2 = [sm[0]];
          for (let k2 = 0; k2 < sm.length - 1; k2++) {
            const p = sm[k2], q = sm[k2 + 1];
            nx2.push({ x: p.x * 0.75 + q.x * 0.25, y: p.y * 0.75 + q.y * 0.25 });
            nx2.push({ x: p.x * 0.25 + q.x * 0.75, y: p.y * 0.25 + q.y * 0.75 });
          }
          nx2.push(sm[sm.length - 1]);
          sm = nx2;
        }
        sm.forEach(p => others.push(p));
        routes.push({ si: pr.si, note: pr.note, pts: sm });
      });
      this._strandCache[cacheKey] = routes;
    }
    const trioFold = (st.selTrio || []).map(n => this.slug(n));
    if (needStrands) (this._strandCache[cacheKey] || []).forEach((rt) => {
      const si = rt.si;
      const cn = this.data('CONNECTIONS')[si];
      const ends = [this.slug(String(cn[0]).split(':')[0]), this.slug(String(cn[1]).split(':')[0])];
      if (!ends.some(e2 => trioFold.indexOf(e2) > -1)) return;
      const on = st.selLink === si || st.hoverLink === si;
      const fade = (st.selLink != null || st.hoverLink != null) && !on ? 0.25 : 1;
      const colour = on ? '#b4762a' : 'rgba(214,138,64,0.75)';
      const pts = rt.pts;
      for (let k = 0; k < pts.length - 1; k++) {
        const p = pts[k], q = pts[k + 1];
        const len = Math.hypot(q.x - p.x, q.y - p.y);
        if (len < 0.5) continue;
        strandWires.push({ key: 's' + si + '-' + k, style: {
          position: 'absolute', left: p.x + 'px', top: p.y + 'px',
          width: (len + 0.7) + 'px', height: 0,
          borderTop: (on ? '1.5px' : '1px') + ' solid ' + colour,
          transform: 'rotate(' + (Math.atan2(q.y - p.y, q.x - p.x) * 180 / Math.PI).toFixed(2) + 'deg)',
          transformOrigin: '0 0', zIndex: 3, opacity: fade, pointerEvents: 'none'
        } });
      }
      for (let k = 2; k < pts.length - 2; k += 6) {
        const p = pts[k];
        strandHits.push({ key: 'h' + si + '-' + k, title: rt.note,
          click: (e) => { if (e && e.stopPropagation) e.stopPropagation(); this.setState({ selLink: this.state.selLink === si ? null : si, sel: null }); },
          enter: () => this.hoverEnter({ hoverLink: si }),
          leave: () => this.setState({ hoverLink: null }),
          style: {
            position: 'absolute', left: (p.x - 9) + 'px', top: (p.y - 9) + 'px',
            width: '18px', height: '18px', borderRadius: '50%', zIndex: 4,
            background: 'transparent', cursor: 'pointer', pointerEvents: 'auto'
          } });
      }
    });

    // ── Which exact FACES does the current hover light? ────────────────────
    // Resolved once per render into instance keys ('vol:drop:isCurator'), so
    // every consumer — cell opacity, face opacity, disc pop, tether colour —
    // asks the same question and cannot disagree. A plain name resolves to its
    // canonical appearance (the one-face rule); a QUALIFIED name
    // (name:vol:drop:role) resolves to that exact face, which is how the Marisa
    // crossing lights both of hers.
    const hcAll = st.pillHover != null ? this.data('CONNECTIONS')[st.pillHover] : null;
    const hoverRawAll = hcAll ? [hcAll[0], hcAll[1], hcAll[2]].filter(Boolean).map(String) : null;
    // Keyed by NAME, not by role: two invitees share a role, so a role key lit
    // both and invented a third person in every two-artist connection.
    const fKey = (vi2, di2, nm) => vi2 + ':' + di2 + ':' + this.slug(nm);
    const litFaces = {};
    if (hoverRawAll) hoverRawAll.forEach(raw => {
      const parts = raw.split(':');
      if (parts.length === 4) { litFaces[fKey(+parts[1] - 1, +parts[2] - 1, parts[0])] = 1; return; }
      const dl = this.dupeIndex()[this.slug(parts[0])] || [];
      if (!dl.length) return;
      const exception = dl.filter(x => !x.isC && x.vi === st.selVi && x.di === st.selDi);
      const pick = exception.length ? exception[0] : (dl.filter(x => x.isC)[0] || dl[0]);
      litFaces[fKey(pick.vi, pick.di, parts[0])] = 1;
    });
    const hoverActive = !!hoverRawAll;

    return this.data('VOLS').map((v, vi) => {
      const wires = [], people = [];
      // One CELL per drop: its wires, its three faces, and its own scale — the
      // whole tree grows as a unit and keeps its relational geometry, instead
      // of each disc inflating into its neighbours.
      const cells = [];
      const pos = volPos[vi];
      const tether = (fromX, fromY, toX, toY, on2, flip2) => {
        const w = Math.max(6, Math.abs(toX - fromX)), h = Math.max(6, Math.abs(toY - fromY));
        // Very light grey when not lit, so an unselected tree recedes as far
        // as its faces and names do.
        const colour2 = on2 ? 'var(--gold)' : 'rgba(0,0,0,0.11)';
        // Reading a connection is about the circles: every tether on the map
        // disappears while a connection dot is hovered.
        if (hoverActive || st.roleFilter) return { display: 'none' };
        const st2 = {
          position: 'absolute', width: w + 'px', height: h + 'px', zIndex: 1,
          pointerEvents: 'none', opacity: on2 ? 1 : 0.9,
          left: Math.min(fromX, toX) + 'px', top: Math.min(fromY, toY) + 'px'
        };
        const right = toX >= fromX;
        if (right) { st2.borderLeft = '1px solid ' + colour2; st2.borderBottom = '1px solid ' + colour2; st2.borderBottomLeftRadius = '100% 100%'; }
        else { st2.borderRight = '1px solid ' + colour2; st2.borderBottom = '1px solid ' + colour2; st2.borderBottomRightRadius = '100% 100%'; }
        if (flip2) {
          st2.borderBottom = 'none';
          st2.borderTop = '1px solid ' + colour2;
          if (right) { st2.borderBottomLeftRadius = 0; st2.borderTopLeftRadius = '100% 100%'; }
          else { st2.borderBottomRightRadius = 0; st2.borderTopRightRadius = '100% 100%'; }
        }
        return st2;
      };

      v.drops.forEach((d, i) => {
        const c = pos[i];
        const cName = d[1], guests = d[3];
        // Gated on the drop that was actually CLICKED, not on the name: a name
        // that appears twice (Marisa Anderson) must never magnify both of its
        // trees. Falls back to a name match only when no drop was recorded.
        const onFan = st.sel != null && (st.selVi != null
          ? (vi === st.selVi && i === st.selDi)
          : (st.sel === cName || guests.indexOf(st.sel) > -1));
        // Every lighting question is answered from litFaces by exact identity.
        const faceLit = (nm) => !!litFaces[fKey(vi, i, nm)];
        const litOf = (nm) => hoverActive ? faceLit(nm) : !!onFan;
        const cellLit = hoverActive && [cName].concat(guests).some(n2 => faceLit(n2));
        const active = hoverActive ? cellLit : !!onFan;
        // Cell magnification belongs to the CLICKED drop alone. Hovering a
        // connection dot must not open another whole tree — it pops just the
        // connected circles (see the disc's own transform).
        const magnified = !!onFan;
        const anyFocus = !!(hoverActive || st.sel);
        const key = (n) => n + '|' + kind + '|' + vi + '|' + i;
        const flipPair = !drift && i % 2 === 1;
        const cellWires = [];
        // A magnified cell REPOSITIONS: the lower artist rises to the upper
        // one's level so the pair reads as one horizontal row, and the tethers
        // (derived from these points) redraw to match. At rest they stay
        // staggered, which is what makes the band feel drifted.
        const gPos = magnified && drift
          ? [
              // A COMPACTED version of the resting arrangement — same two tiers,
              // both artists drawn up closer to the curator so the magnified
              // cell takes less vertical room. Not levelled: the stagger is the
              // shape of the thing.
              { x: c.x - 30, y: c.y + Math.round(A_DY * 0.72) },
              { x: c.x + 31, y: c.y + Math.round(B_DY * 0.56) }
            ]
          : [
              { x: c.x + (drift ? -30 : (flipPair ? 34 : -34)), y: c.y + (drift ? A_DY : (flipPair ? B_DY : A_DY)) },
              { x: c.x + (drift ? 34 : (flipPair ? -34 : 34)), y: c.y + (drift ? B_DY : (flipPair ? A_DY : B_DY)) }
            ];
        guests.forEach((g, gi) => {
          const p = gPos[gi];
          cellWires.push({ key: 'w' + i + gi, style: tether(c.x, c.y + (p.y > c.y ? C / 2 : -C / 2), p.x, p.y + (p.y > c.y ? -I / 2 : I / 2), litOf(cName) && litOf(g), p.y < c.y) });
        });
        const mk = (name, isC, p) => {
          const pending = /pending/i.test(name);
          // This exact face's lit state — resolved globally, so a remote
          // partner lights while its drop-mates stay dark, and a duplicated
          // name still lights only one of its appearances.
          const hoverPair = hoverActive ? true : null;
          const litByPill = faceLit(name);
          // A role filter recedes the other kind of face.
          const roleDim = !!st.roleFilter && ((st.roleFilter === 'c') !== isC);
          // How far the disc grows PAST its layout box — the name has to clear
          // this, since a transform doesn't move anything around it.
          const popScale = litByPill ? (magnified ? 1.15 : 1.42) : 1;
          const popPad = Math.round((isC ? C : I) * (popScale - 1) / 2) + (litByPill ? 7 : 0);
          // Only the top of a Volume Two cell — the curator — swings its name to
          // the right, and it does so whenever it is highlighted, magnified or
          // lit as someone's connection partner.
          // A magnified curator always puts its name BESIDE the face, never
          // above it: it frees the space over the band and reads better. The
          // band's last drop has no room to its right, so that one mirrors to
          // the left rather than running off the canvas.
          // The right-hand invited artist also puts its name BESIDE the circle
          // (Marisa Anderson in Drop 2), so it stops landing on the quote below.
          const nameAside = (isC || p.gi === 1) && (magnified || litByPill);
          const nameLeft = nameAside && isC && i === v.drops.length - 1;
          const nameRight = nameAside && !nameLeft;
          const sel = hoverActive ? litByPill : (st.sel === name && onFan);
          const hov = st.mnhover === key(name);
          const dim = hoverPair ? !litByPill : (st.sel && !onFan);
          const popped = litByPill || (onFan && !hoverPair);
          // No per-face growth: magnification is the CELL's transform, so the
          // faces, the tethers and the names all scale together and never
          // collide. A face only reacts in weight, not size.
          const grow = 0;
          const size = (isC ? C : I) + grow;
          const boxW = isC ? 116 : 96;
          return {
            key: (isC ? 'c' : 'g') + i + (p.gi == null ? '' : p.gi),
            name: name,
            // §4-7 · see the phone map's note: slug(), not rendered text.
            handle: this.slug(name),
            photo: this.photoFor(name) || '',
            initials: this.initials(name) || '·',
            // Volume Two's top row grows UPWARD into the connections field, so
            // while magnified its names sit to the RIGHT of the face instead of
            // above it — a positioning change only, nothing else moves.
            wrap: isC && drift && !nameAside
              ? {
                  position: 'absolute', left: (p.x - boxW / 2) + 'px',
                  bottom: (H - (p.y + size / 2)) + 'px',
                  width: boxW + 'px', display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column-reverse', alignItems: 'center', gap: (5 + popPad) + 'px', pointerEvents: 'none',
                  zIndex: litByPill ? 9 : (sel || hov ? 7 : 5), cursor: pending ? 'default' : 'pointer',
                  // Only faces INSIDE a lit cell dim individually — an unlit
                  // cell is already at 0.09, and multiplying the two wiped the
                  // ghosted background out entirely.
                  opacity: roleDim ? 0.12 : ((hoverPair && cellLit && !litByPill) ? 0.1 : 1),
                  transition: 'opacity 200ms ease, bottom 260ms cubic-bezier(.34,1.4,.64,1)'
                }
              : {
                  position: 'absolute',
                  // Name beside the face: to the right normally, mirrored to the
                  // left on the band's last drop so it stays on the canvas.
                  left: (nameLeft ? (p.x + size / 2 - (size + 6 + boxW))
                        : nameRight ? (p.x - size / 2)
                        : (p.x - boxW / 2)) + 'px',
                  top: (p.y - size / 2) + 'px',
                  width: (nameAside ? (size + 6 + boxW) : boxW) + 'px',
                  display: 'flex',
                  flexDirection: nameLeft ? 'row-reverse' : (nameRight ? 'row' : 'column'),
                  alignItems: 'center', gap: (nameAside ? 6 : 5) + popPad + 'px', pointerEvents: 'none',
                  zIndex: litByPill ? 9 : (sel || hov ? 7 : 5), cursor: pending ? 'default' : 'pointer',
                  opacity: roleDim ? 0.12 : ((hoverPair && cellLit && !litByPill) ? 0.1 : 1),
                  transition: 'opacity 200ms ease, top 260ms cubic-bezier(.34,1.4,.64,1)'
                },
            disc: {
              // position: relative so the hover-play invitation can sit on the
              // disc's own edge without leaving the flow (2026-09-04).
              position: 'relative',
              width: size + 'px', height: size + 'px', flexShrink: 0, boxSizing: 'border-box',
              pointerEvents: 'auto', cursor: pending ? 'default' : 'pointer',
              transition: 'width 260ms cubic-bezier(.34,1.4,.64,1), height 260ms cubic-bezier(.34,1.4,.64,1), box-shadow 200ms ease, transform 220ms cubic-bezier(.34,1.4,.64,1)',
              // A hovered connection enlarges ONLY the connected circles.
              transform: popScale === 1 ? 'none' : 'scale(' + popScale + ')',
              borderRadius: isC ? '50%' : (drift ? '50%' : '7px'),
              border: (sel || hov ? 3 : 2) + 'px solid ' + (hoverPair && cellLit && !litByPill ? 'rgba(0,0,0,0.10)' : (sel || hov ? 'var(--gold)' : isC ? '#b4762a' : '#2f8f86')),
              background: (pending || !this.photoFor(name)) ? 'var(--tile)' : ('url(' + this.photoFor(name) + ') center/cover no-repeat, var(--tile)'),
              boxShadow: litByPill ? '0 3px 10px rgba(0,0,0,0.13), 0 0 0 4px rgba(219,191,61,0.34)' : (sel ? '0 3px 10px rgba(0,0,0,0.12), 0 0 0 3px rgba(219,191,61,0.24)' : (popped ? '0 3px 9px rgba(0,0,0,0.10)' : 'none')),
              display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center',
              opacity: pending ? 0.5 : 1
            },
            monogram: pending ? { fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace", fontSize: '10px', color: 'var(--ink40)' } : { display: 'none' },
            // ── NO PLAY BUTTON ON A FACE (2026-09-05, Tom: "these should be
            // mouseover audio. There should be no play button.") ─────────────
            // ⚠ THIS WAS MINE AND IT WAS AN INVENTION. In r6 I read "hovering
            // a curator plays their track" as undiscoverable and added a ▶
            // badge to advertise it. But the map's gesture IS the hover — you
            // sweep the composition and it sounds — and a button on a face
            // says "press me to play", which is a different, wrong promise on
            // a surface where the sound has already started by the time you
            // could read it. The hover-audio switch is the affordance; it is
            // armed by default on this route. Kept as a false constant so the
            // markup does not have to change and so the reason survives.
            // ══ PLAYER MODE PUTS A BUTTON ON THE CIRCLE (2026-09-09, Tom) ══════
            // "In player mode I need a play button to show up when somebody
            // hovers over the circle… they hit play, that track plays, but no
            // transport pops up because there's no room for it on the curator
            // map. The play button turns to pause, and that keeps playing until
            // somebody hits pause. They can move their mouse off and it keeps
            // playing."
            //
            // This was a permanent `display: none` stub with a literal ▶︎ behind
            // it — drawn, never shown, and unreachable.
            //
            // IT SHOWS ON TWO CONDITIONS, and the second is the one that makes
            // his description work: the cursor is on this circle, OR this circle
            // is the one sounding. Hover alone would take the pause away the
            // moment the mouse left, and there would be no way to stop it — so
            // the sounding circle keeps its ❙❙ with the cursor anywhere. That is
            // also what lets someone play every track in a row: each circle
            // offers ▶︎ on approach, and the one playing is always the one
            // showing ❙❙.
            //
            // NEVER IN HOVER MODE. playCtl() gates it, so the curator map obeys
            // the site-wide rule — no play buttons anywhere in hover mode — and
            // hover mode's behaviour here is untouched: hover audio per circle,
            // exactly as it has been.
            //
            // ⚠ NO TRANSPORT, AND THAT NEEDS NOTHING NEW. ath-map is already
            // excluded from hasTransportNow in both modes (Tom, 2026-09-03: the
            // map never shows the bar), so a press here sounds without raising
            // one. This button IS the transport for this surface, which is why
            // it has to carry the pause.
            // ⚠ AND NOT WHERE THERE IS NOTHING TO PLAY. recFor(name) is null for a
            // curator with no PAIRS row and no matchable drop — 'pan-american'
            // measured null — and this.play(null) sets `started` with no record:
            // a press that appears to work, sounds nothing, and can never show
            // its own pause because mapSeatOn compares npPlay to that same null.
            //
            // The button is absent in that case rather than dead. Same reasoning
            // as hasAudio:false and as rule 5 for addresses — a circle that is
            // not a control beats a control that does nothing, and it is visible
            // in a way an inert button is not. It is also the same identity hover
            // mode uses (catHover: recFor(name)), so a circle silent under the
            // cursor in hover mode has no button in player mode: the two modes
            // agree about which circles have audio.
            hoverPlay: this.playCtl((hov || this.mapSeatOn(st, key(name), name)) && !pending && this.recFor(name) != null
              ? {
                  // ── ON THE EDGE, NOT OVER THE FACE (2026-09-09, Tom) ────────
                  // "Before, we had the player in the corner, so it didn't come
                  // over the picture. It appeared sort of on the edge — a smaller
                  // circle intersecting the edge line of the bubble."
                  //
                  // My first pass centred it, which covered the photograph — the
                  // thing the circle exists to show. 85.36% is the disc's own
                  // perimeter on the lower-right diagonal (50% + 35.36%, which is
                  // r·cos45° as a percentage of the diameter), so the badge sits
                  // CENTRED ON THE EDGE LINE and straddles it whatever size the
                  // disc is — curators, invited artists, magnified or not. A
                  // fixed offset would drift the moment a disc changed size, and
                  // they change size on hover.
                  //
                  // 20px against the old 26: smaller because it is now beside the
                  // picture rather than instead of it. The 44px tapPad underneath
                  // still carries touch.
                  // ── IT IS A SMALL SEAT, NOT A VIDEO BADGE (2026-09-09, Tom) ─
                  // "It was a little more stylized… the circle was stylized like
                  // the artist circle with the same border."
                  //
                  // Right — I reached for the dark translucent scrim this file
                  // uses on PHOTOGRAPHS (the news video tiles, the radio tile),
                  // which is the vocabulary for a badge sitting ON an image. On
                  // the curator map the circles ARE the vocabulary: a cream fill
                  // with a 2px warm border. So the button is a smaller one of
                  // those — same fill, same border weight, gold ink — and reads
                  // as a satellite of the seat rather than an overlay on it.
                  //
                  // ⚠ AND THE GLYPH NEEDED CENTRING BY HAND. ▶ carries a large
                  // left side bearing, so flex centring puts its INK low and
                  // left of the circle's middle even though its BOX is centred —
                  // visible in Tom's screenshot. The 1px indent and the tuned
                  // line-height correct the ink, which is the thing being
                  // looked at. ❙❙ is symmetrical and needs neither, but a shared
                  // style cannot branch on the glyph, so the compensation is
                  // small enough to be invisible on the pause mark.
                  position: 'absolute', left: '85.36%', top: '85.36%',
                  width: '19px', height: '19px', margin: '-9.5px 0 0 -9.5px',
                  borderRadius: '50%', boxSizing: 'border-box',
                  // var(--tile), not var(--bg): the seats' own fill for a circle
                  // with no photograph is the cream tile, and on ATH's light
                  // theme --bg is white — near enough to look like a mistake
                  // rather than a match. The border stays 2px: the disc's is 3px
                  // while hovered, which is the only time this is visible, but 3
                  // on a 19px circle is a ring rather than an edge.
                  background: 'var(--tile)', border: '2px solid var(--gold)',
                  zIndex: 3,
                  display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center',
                  cursor: 'pointer', pointerEvents: 'auto'
                }
              : { display: 'none' }),
              // ── THE MARK IS DRAWN, NOT TYPED (2026-09-09, Tom) ─────────────
              // ⚠ THREE PASSES AT CENTRING A CHARACTER, ALL WRONG. The glyph was
              // U+25B6 U+FE0E — a text-presentation triangle with asymmetric
              // side bearings AND a variation selector carrying its own advance.
              // So the text BOX centred while the ink did not, my measurements
              // read the box and reported success, and a 1px padding
              // "correction" pushed it further right. There was no font metric
              // to tune: the metric was the problem.
              //
              // Drawn instead. The triangle is a CSS border triangle (6×8, an
              // exact box, no font involved) and the pause is two 2px bars from
              // one element's left and right borders. Both boxes are known
              // integers, so flex centring is geometric and identical at every
              // disc size and every deck scale.
              //
              // pointerEvents: none, so a press always lands on the badge that
              // owns the handler rather than on the mark inside it.
              playMark: this.mapSeatOn(st, key(name), name)
                ? { width: '6px', height: '8px', boxSizing: 'border-box',
                    borderLeft: '2px solid var(--gold)', borderRight: '2px solid var(--gold)',
                    pointerEvents: 'none' }
                : { width: 0, height: 0,
                    borderStyle: 'solid', borderWidth: '4px 0 4px 6px',
                    borderColor: 'transparent transparent transparent var(--gold)',
                    pointerEvents: 'none' },
            // stopPropagation, or the disc's own click would select the face at
            // the same time — a press is about audio, a tap on the disc is about
            // the composition, and they are two different intents on one dot.
            playClick: (e) => {
              if (e && e.stopPropagation) e.stopPropagation();
              if (pending) return;
              // ⚠ AFTER play(), NOT BEFORE. play() clears mapSeat — that is how a
              // press on any other surface releases the seat — so claiming it
              // first meant the map's own press wiped its own claim. The seat,
              // so the pause lands on the circle that was pressed and not on
              // every circle sharing its record. See mapSeatOn.
              // ── AND IT PLAYS THIS SEAT'S TRACK (2026-09-09, Tom: "Marisa
              // Anderson is seated twice, but she has two different tracks") ──
              // A seat is a PERSON ON A DROP, not a person: the curator is track
              // 0 of their drop and each guest is 1 or 2, so the same face in two
              // places is two different recordings. This passed no track at all,
              // so both of her circles played whatever recFor(name) resolved to
              // first — one track, twice.
              //
              // ⚠ AND THE DATA STILL CANNOT SAY IT COMPLETELY. recFor(name)
              // flattens to ONE PAIRS index per name (its ATH fallback matches
              // the drop loosely), so two seats differ here only by their
              // position in the trio. Two seats of the same person at the same
              // position on different drops remain indistinguishable to this
              // file — that needs a per-seat address from engineering, the same
              // shape as PAIRS[7] for albums. Recorded rather than guessed.
              this.play(this.recFor(name), isC ? 0 : ((p && p.gi != null ? p.gi : 0) + 1));
              this.setState({ mapSeat: key(name) });
            },
            nameStyle: {
              // Smaller while magnified: the cell's own scale already makes it
              // large, so the type steps down to stay in proportion.
              pointerEvents: 'auto', cursor: pending ? 'default' : 'pointer',
              fontSize: magnified ? (isC ? '8px' : '7.5px') : (isC ? '10.5px' : '9.5px'),
              fontWeight: 400, lineHeight: 1.25,
              textAlign: nameLeft ? 'right' : (nameRight ? 'left' : 'center'),
              width: nameAside ? 'auto' : '100%',
              flexShrink: nameAside ? 0 : 1,
              color: sel || hov ? 'var(--gold)' : isC ? 'var(--ink)' : 'var(--ink70)',
              // A magnified cell sits over its ghosted neighbours; the halo keeps
              // its names clean where they cross one.
              position: 'relative', zIndex: 4,
              textShadow: onFan || cellLit || litByPill ? '0 0 4px var(--bg), 0 0 8px var(--bg), 0 0 14px var(--bg), 0 0 20px var(--bg)' : 'none',
              fontStyle: pending ? 'italic' : 'normal', overflowWrap: 'break-word'
            },
            // ── A FACE IS A RECORD, SO HOVERING ONE PLAYS IT (2026-09-04) ──────
            // The map's faces set only mnhover — the lit-name state — so hover
            // audio was on by default here with nothing to act on: the map was
            // the one catalogue surface where moving across it made no sound.
            // catHover is the preview target the host reads, exactly as the
            // star field and the catalogue grid set it; recFor(name) is the
            // resolver, and a curator with no record under their own name
            // resolves through their drop. The transport stays down on this
            // route (hasTransport), so this is sound without chrome — which is
            // the whole point of the map.
            enter: () => {
              if (pending) return;
              this.setState({
                mnhover: key(name),
                catHover: this.recFor(name),
                catHoverName: name,
                catHoverTitle: ''
              });
            },
            leave: () => this.setState({ mnhover: null, catHover: null, catHoverName: null, catHoverTitle: null }),
            // ── THE SECOND CALL SITE (2026-09-04, block B1) ────────────────
            // The pad and open-or-keep landed in the structured builder only;
            // this is the drift path, whose dots are 27–38px. Same change,
            // because "which builder drew it" is not something a finger knows.
            // The pad is transparent and centred on the disc, so the dot's
            // DRAWN size stays the composition's.
            tapPad: pending ? { display: 'none' } : {
              position: 'absolute', left: '50%', top: '50%',
              width: '44px', height: '44px', margin: '-22px 0 0 -22px',
              borderRadius: '50%', background: 'transparent', zIndex: 1
            },
            click: (e) => {
              if (e && e.stopPropagation) e.stopPropagation();
              if (pending) return;
              // Open-or-keep: a tap's synthetic hover must not be undone by its
              // own click. Only a second tap on the already-selected face
              // clears the selection.
              if (st.sel === name) { this.setState({ roleFilter: null, sel: null, selLink: null, selTrio: null, selDrop: null, selTrack: null, selCurator: null, selKind: null, selVi: null, selDi: null }); return; }
              this.setState({ roleFilter: null, sel: name, selLink: null, selTrio: [cName].concat(guests), selDrop: v.label + ' · Drop ' + d[0], selTrack: d[2], selCurator: cName, selKind: isC ? 'c' : 'a', selVi: vi, selDi: i });
            }
          };
        };
        const cellPeople = [mk(cName, true, { x: c.x, y: c.y })];
        guests.forEach((g, gi) => cellPeople.push(mk(g, false, { x: gPos[gi].x, y: gPos[gi].y, gi: gi })));
        // Kept a touch under 2× so a scaled cell cannot climb out of its band
        // into the neighbouring volume's names.
        const k = magnified ? 1.7 : 1;
        cells.push({
          key: 'cell' + i,
          fanStyle: {
            position: 'absolute', left: 0, top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0,
            // The leftmost and rightmost cells would project past the shell's
            // clip at 1.7×, cutting a name mid-word. Their origin is nudged
            // toward that edge so the growth happens inward.
            // The origin is nudged AWAY from every edge the cell could grow past,
            // because the page shell clips (which reads as a straight-edged
            // "mask" across a neighbouring circle). Horizontally for the first
            // and last drop; vertically for the last band, whose tree would
            // otherwise project below its own band.
            transformOrigin: (i === 0 ? Math.max(0, c.x - 26) : (i === v.drops.length - 1 ? c.x + 20 : c.x)) + 'px '
              + (vi === this.data('VOLS').length - 1 ? c.y + 40 : c.y) + 'px',
            // A curator with a big clump of dots sits right under it once
            // magnified — Chuck Johnson especially — so a busy Volume Two cell
            // steps down far enough that the dots stay reachable.
            transform: 'translateY(' + (magnified ? ((vi === this.data('VOLS').length - 1) ? 26 : ((vp[0] >= 1728 && i >= 5) ? 32 : 0)) : 0) + 'px) scale(' + k + ')',
            zIndex: active ? 9 : 1,
            opacity: anyFocus && !active ? 0.09 : 1,
            pointerEvents: 'none',
            transition: 'transform 300ms cubic-bezier(.34,1.35,.64,1), opacity 220ms ease'
          },
          drop: '', dropStyle: { display: 'none' },
          wires: cellWires, hits: [], people: cellPeople
        });
      });

      return {
        key: 'fb' + vi,
        label: v.label, year: vi === 0 ? '2025' : '2026',
        note: 'eight curators · twenty-four artists',
        bandStyle: { position: 'relative', marginTop: vi === 0 ? '10px' : (st.selTrio && st.selTrio.length ? '18px' : '6px'), transition: 'margin-top 320ms cubic-bezier(.4,0,.2,1)' },
        gridStyle: { marginTop: '4px' },
        // Just a quiet marker under the band's first drop — no year, no counts.
        // Tucked in beside the first drop's lower artist, not floating above
        // the blob field.
        // Up beside the band's first curator, in the empty space to its left.
        // Hidden while Drop 1 is magnified: the marker lives at the first
        // curator's own level, so a 1.7× Drop 1 would land its name on top.
        footStyle: {
          position: 'absolute', left: '0px',
          top: (((pos[0] && pos[0].y) || BASE) - C / 2 - 52) + 'px',
          display: 'flex', alignItems: 'baseline',
          opacity: st.sel != null ? 0 : 1,
          transition: 'opacity 200ms ease'
        },
        footLabel: 'Vol. ' + (vi + 1) + ' · ' + (vi === 0 ? '2025' : '2026') + ' · Drops 1–8',
        footLabelStyle: { fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace", fontSize: '10px', fontWeight: 700, letterSpacing: '0.16em', textTransform: 'uppercase', color: 'var(--ink70)' },
        panelAfter: vi === 0,
        groups: [{
          key: 'fg' + vi,
          groupStyle: { position: 'relative', height: H + 'px', minWidth: 0 },
          links: [],
          fans: cells.concat(vi === 0 && (strandWires.length || strandHits.length) ? [{
            key: 'ffx',
            drop: '', dropStyle: { display: 'none' },
            fanStyle: { position: 'absolute', left: 0, top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, pointerEvents: 'none' },
            wires: strandWires, hits: strandHits, people: []
          }] : [])
        }]
      };
    });
  }

  buildMap(mode, W, st) {
    const M = this.MODES[mode];
    if (M.dir === 'free') return this.buildFree(M.kind, W, st);
    const avail = W - 48;
    const wire = (x, y, w, h, on, dotted) => ({
      position: 'absolute', left: x + 'px', top: y + 'px',
      width: (w || 0) + 'px', height: (h || 0) + 'px', zIndex: dotted ? 2 : 1,
      borderTop: h ? 'none' : (dotted ? '2px dotted ' : '1px solid ') + (on ? 'var(--gold)' : dotted ? 'rgba(138,106,18,0.55)' : 'var(--ink40)'),
      borderLeft: h ? (dotted ? '2px dotted ' : '1px solid ') + (on ? 'var(--gold)' : dotted ? 'rgba(138,106,18,0.55)' : 'var(--ink40)') : 'none',
      opacity: on ? 1 : (dotted ? 0.75 : 0.5), pointerEvents: 'none'
    });

    return this.data('VOLS').map((v, vi) => {
      const perRow = M.perRowFn(W) * M.colsPerVol;
      const bandCols = M.dir === 'v' ? M.colsPerVol : 1;
      const volW = M.dir === 'v'
        ? Math.floor((avail - (this.data('VOLS').length - 1) * 40) / this.data('VOLS').length)
        : avail;
      const cellW = M.dir === 'v'
        ? Math.floor((volW - (bandCols - 1) * M.gap) / bandCols)
        : Math.floor((avail - (M.perRowFn(W) - 1) * M.gap) / M.perRowFn(W));

      // Fan geometry per direction.
      const G = M.dir === 'v'
        ? { CX: Math.round(M.c / 2) + 4, CY: 30, MIDX: Math.round(cellW * 0.34), IX: Math.round(cellW * 0.46),
            // DEAD BRANCH REMOVED 2026-09-01. These read M.tight, but MODES defines
            // no tight key on any of its five modes and M is never spread or
            // assigned to, so M.tight was always undefined and every one of these
            // took its false branch. Collapsed to the values that were actually
            // rendering, so the file stops advertising a curator-map tight mode that
            // never existed. The phone map's response to a short frame is the
            // measured wrapH + fitK pass (see phoneMap), NOT a tight branch.
            IYA: 12, IYB: 12 + M.i + 14,
            H: M.i * 2 + 56, GUT: 8 }
        : { CX: Math.round(cellW / 2), CY: Math.round(M.c / 2) + 4, SPINE: M.c + 44,
            IXA: Math.round(cellW * 0.26), IXB: Math.round(cellW * 0.74),
            IYA: M.c + 54, IYB: M.c + 54 + (M.stagger || 0), H: M.c + 54 + (M.stagger || 0) + M.i + 40, CH: 30 };
      const STRIDE = G.H + (M.dir === 'v' ? 10 : 22); // was (M.tight ? 6 : 10) — dead, see above

      const groups = [];
      for (let gi2 = 0; gi2 < M.colsPerVol; gi2++) {
        const drops = v.drops.slice(gi2 * M.chunk, (gi2 + 1) * M.chunk);
        const rowsPerGroup = M.dir === 'v' ? drops.length : Math.ceil(drops.length / M.perRowFn(W));

        const links = [];
        drops.forEach((d, di) => {
          d[3].forEach((g, gj) => {
            const ci = drops.findIndex(x => x[1] === g);
            if (ci < 0) return;
            const on = st.sel === g;
            const fade = st.sel && !on ? 0.22 : 1;
            if (M.dir === 'v') {
              const fromY = di * STRIDE + (gj === 0 ? G.IYA : G.IYB) + M.i / 2;
              const toY = ci * STRIDE + G.CY;
              const w1 = G.IX - M.i / 2 - G.GUT;
              links.push({ key: 'l' + di + gj + 'a', style: Object.assign(wire(G.GUT, fromY, w1, 0, on, true), { opacity: fade }) });
              links.push({ key: 'l' + di + gj + 'b', style: Object.assign(wire(G.GUT, Math.min(fromY, toY), 0, Math.abs(toY - fromY), on, true), { opacity: fade }) });
              links.push({ key: 'l' + di + gj + 'c', style: Object.assign(wire(G.GUT, toY, G.CX - M.c / 2 - G.GUT, 0, on, true), { opacity: fade }) });
            } else {
              const per = M.perRowFn(W);
              const pos = (i, kind, k) => {
                const row = Math.floor(i / per), col = i % per;
                const base = col * (cellW + M.gap);
                return kind === 'c'
                  ? { x: base + G.CX, y: G.CH + row * STRIDE + G.CY }
                  : { x: base + (k === 0 ? G.IXA : G.IXB), y: G.CH + row * STRIDE + (k === 0 ? G.IYA : G.IYB) + M.i / 2 };
              };
              const from = pos(di, 'a', gj), to = pos(ci, 'c', null);
              const chY = 14, x1 = Math.min(from.x, to.x), x2 = Math.max(from.x, to.x);
              links.push({ key: 'l' + di + gj + 'a', style: Object.assign(wire(from.x, chY, 0, from.y - chY, on, true), { opacity: fade }) });
              links.push({ key: 'l' + di + gj + 'b', style: {
                position: 'absolute', left: x1 + 'px', top: (chY - 18) + 'px', width: (x2 - x1) + 'px', height: '18px', zIndex: 2,
                borderTop: '2px dotted ' + (on ? 'var(--gold)' : 'rgba(138,106,18,0.55)'),
                borderLeft: '2px dotted ' + (on ? 'var(--gold)' : 'rgba(138,106,18,0.55)'),
                borderRight: '2px dotted ' + (on ? 'var(--gold)' : 'rgba(138,106,18,0.55)'),
                borderTopLeftRadius: '50% 100%', borderTopRightRadius: '50% 100%',
                opacity: fade, pointerEvents: 'none' } });
              links.push({ key: 'l' + di + gj + 'c', style: Object.assign(wire(to.x, chY, 0, to.y - chY - M.c / 2, on, true), { opacity: fade }) });
            }
          });
        });

        groups.push({
          key: 'g' + vi + gi2,
          groupStyle: M.dir === 'v'
            ? { position: 'relative', minWidth: 0 }
            : { position: 'relative', minWidth: 0, paddingTop: G.CH + 'px', display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(' + M.perRowFn(W) + ', minmax(0, 1fr))', columnGap: M.gap + 'px', rowGap: '22px' },
          links: links,
          fans: drops.map((d, di) => {
            const cName = d[1], guests = d[3];
            const onFan = st.sel && (st.sel === cName || guests.indexOf(st.sel) > -1);
        // Derived BEFORE the tethers are built, so a line's colour can follow
        // the same lighting its two faces do.
        const hcC = st.pillHover != null ? this.data('CONNECTIONS')[st.pillHover] : null;
        const hoverNames = hcC ? [hcC[0], hcC[1], hcC[2]].filter(Boolean).map(n => this.slug(String(n).split(':')[0])) : null;
        const litOf = (n) => hoverNames ? hoverNames.indexOf(this.slug(n)) > -1 : !!onFan;
        const cellLit = !!(hoverNames && [cName].concat(guests).some(n => litOf(n)));
        const active = hoverNames ? cellLit : !!onFan;
        const anyFocus = !!(hoverNames || st.sel);
            const key = (name) => name + '|' + mode + '|' + vi + '|' + gi2 + '|' + di;
            const person = (name, kind, k) => {
              const pending = /pending/i.test(name);
              const sel = st.sel === name;
              const hov = st.mnhover === key(name);
              const dim = st.sel && !onFan;
              const size = kind === 'c' ? M.c : M.i;
              const cx = M.dir === 'v' ? (kind === 'c' ? G.CX : G.IX) : (kind === 'c' ? G.CX : (k === 0 ? G.IXA : G.IXB));
              const cy = M.dir === 'v'
                ? (kind === 'c' ? G.CY : (k === 0 ? G.IYA : G.IYB) + size / 2)
                : (kind === 'c' ? G.CY : (k === 0 ? G.IYA : G.IYB) + size / 2);
              const besides = M.dir === 'v' && kind === 'a';
              const boxW = besides ? Math.max(120, cellW - G.IX - M.i / 2 - 6) : (M.dir === 'v' ? M.c + 40 : cellW);
              return {
                key: kind + (k == null ? 'c' : k),
                name: name,
                // §4-7 · see the phone map's note: slug(), not rendered text.
                handle: this.slug(name),
                photo: this.photoFor(name) || '',
                initials: this.initials(name) || '·',
                wrap: besides
                  ? { position: 'absolute', left: (cx - size / 2) + 'px', top: (cy - size / 2) + 'px',
                      display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '9px', width: (size + 9 + boxW) + 'px',
                      zIndex: sel || hov ? 7 : 5, cursor: pending ? 'default' : 'pointer',
                      opacity: dim ? 0.26 : 1, transition: 'opacity 160ms ease' }
                  : { position: 'absolute', left: (cx - boxW / 2) + 'px', top: (cy - size / 2) + 'px', width: boxW + 'px',
                      display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', alignItems: 'center', gap: '5px',
                      zIndex: sel || hov ? 7 : 5, cursor: pending ? 'default' : 'pointer',
                      opacity: dim ? 0.26 : 1, transition: 'opacity 160ms ease' },
                disc: {
                  width: size + 'px', height: size + 'px', flexShrink: 0, borderRadius: '50%', boxSizing: 'border-box',
                  border: (sel || hov ? 3 : 2) + 'px solid ' + (sel || hov ? 'var(--gold)' : kind === 'c' ? '#b4762a' : '#2f8f86'),
                  background: (pending || !this.photoFor(name)) ? 'var(--tile)' : ('url(' + this.photoFor(name) + ') center/cover no-repeat, var(--tile)'),
                  boxShadow: litByPill ? '0 14px 34px rgba(0,0,0,0.26), 0 0 0 5px rgba(219,191,61,0.32)' : (sel ? '0 8px 24px rgba(0,0,0,0.18), 0 0 0 4px rgba(219,191,61,0.22)' : (popped ? '0 8px 22px rgba(0,0,0,0.18)' : 'none')),
                  display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center',
                  opacity: pending ? 0.5 : 1
                },
                monogram: pending ? { fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace", fontSize: '10px', color: 'var(--ink40)' } : { display: 'none' },
            // ── NO PLAY BUTTON ON A FACE (2026-09-05, Tom: "these should be
            // mouseover audio. There should be no play button.") ─────────────
            // ⚠ THIS WAS MINE AND IT WAS AN INVENTION. In r6 I read "hovering
            // a curator plays their track" as undiscoverable and added a ▶
            // badge to advertise it. But the map's gesture IS the hover — you
            // sweep the composition and it sounds — and a button on a face
            // says "press me to play", which is a different, wrong promise on
            // a surface where the sound has already started by the time you
            // could read it.
            //
            // ⚠ AND IT IS NOW TRUE OF HOVER MODE ONLY (2026-09-09, Tom). That
            // instruction was given when hover was the only way this map made
            // sound. Player mode is the case it did not cover: there IS no hover
            // audio, so a circle with no button offers nothing at all. The
            // reasoning above still holds exactly where it was aimed — playCtl()
            // keeps the button out of hover mode — and player mode gets the
            // button, because in that mode a press is the only gesture there is.
            //
            // Same treatment as the structured builder's seat; see its note for
            // why the sounding circle keeps its ❙❙ after the cursor leaves.
            // ⚠ AND NOT WHERE THERE IS NOTHING TO PLAY. recFor(name) is null for a
            // curator with no PAIRS row and no matchable drop — 'pan-american'
            // measured null — and this.play(null) sets `started` with no record:
            // a press that appears to work, sounds nothing, and can never show
            // its own pause because mapSeatOn compares npPlay to that same null.
            //
            // The button is absent in that case rather than dead. Same reasoning
            // as hasAudio:false and as rule 5 for addresses — a circle that is
            // not a control beats a control that does nothing, and it is visible
            // in a way an inert button is not. It is also the same identity hover
            // mode uses (catHover: recFor(name)), so a circle silent under the
            // cursor in hover mode has no button in player mode: the two modes
            // agree about which circles have audio.
            hoverPlay: this.playCtl((hov || this.mapSeatOn(st, key(name), name)) && !pending && this.recFor(name) != null
              ? {
                  // ── ON THE EDGE, NOT OVER THE FACE (2026-09-09, Tom) ────────
                  // "Before, we had the player in the corner, so it didn't come
                  // over the picture. It appeared sort of on the edge — a smaller
                  // circle intersecting the edge line of the bubble."
                  //
                  // My first pass centred it, which covered the photograph — the
                  // thing the circle exists to show. 85.36% is the disc's own
                  // perimeter on the lower-right diagonal (50% + 35.36%, which is
                  // r·cos45° as a percentage of the diameter), so the badge sits
                  // CENTRED ON THE EDGE LINE and straddles it whatever size the
                  // disc is — curators, invited artists, magnified or not. A
                  // fixed offset would drift the moment a disc changed size, and
                  // they change size on hover.
                  //
                  // 20px against the old 26: smaller because it is now beside the
                  // picture rather than instead of it. The 44px tapPad underneath
                  // still carries touch.
                  // ── IT IS A SMALL SEAT, NOT A VIDEO BADGE (2026-09-09, Tom) ─
                  // "It was a little more stylized… the circle was stylized like
                  // the artist circle with the same border."
                  //
                  // Right — I reached for the dark translucent scrim this file
                  // uses on PHOTOGRAPHS (the news video tiles, the radio tile),
                  // which is the vocabulary for a badge sitting ON an image. On
                  // the curator map the circles ARE the vocabulary: a cream fill
                  // with a 2px warm border. So the button is a smaller one of
                  // those — same fill, same border weight, gold ink — and reads
                  // as a satellite of the seat rather than an overlay on it.
                  //
                  // ⚠ AND THE GLYPH NEEDED CENTRING BY HAND. ▶ carries a large
                  // left side bearing, so flex centring puts its INK low and
                  // left of the circle's middle even though its BOX is centred —
                  // visible in Tom's screenshot. The 1px indent and the tuned
                  // line-height correct the ink, which is the thing being
                  // looked at. ❙❙ is symmetrical and needs neither, but a shared
                  // style cannot branch on the glyph, so the compensation is
                  // small enough to be invisible on the pause mark.
                  position: 'absolute', left: '85.36%', top: '85.36%',
                  width: '19px', height: '19px', margin: '-9.5px 0 0 -9.5px',
                  borderRadius: '50%', boxSizing: 'border-box',
                  // var(--tile), not var(--bg): the seats' own fill for a circle
                  // with no photograph is the cream tile, and on ATH's light
                  // theme --bg is white — near enough to look like a mistake
                  // rather than a match. The border stays 2px: the disc's is 3px
                  // while hovered, which is the only time this is visible, but 3
                  // on a 19px circle is a ring rather than an edge.
                  background: 'var(--tile)', border: '2px solid var(--gold)',
                  zIndex: 3,
                  display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center',
                  cursor: 'pointer', pointerEvents: 'auto'
                }
              : { display: 'none' }),
              // ── THE MARK IS DRAWN, NOT TYPED (2026-09-09, Tom) ─────────────
              // ⚠ THREE PASSES AT CENTRING A CHARACTER, ALL WRONG. The glyph was
              // U+25B6 U+FE0E — a text-presentation triangle with asymmetric
              // side bearings AND a variation selector carrying its own advance.
              // So the text BOX centred while the ink did not, my measurements
              // read the box and reported success, and a 1px padding
              // "correction" pushed it further right. There was no font metric
              // to tune: the metric was the problem.
              //
              // Drawn instead. The triangle is a CSS border triangle (6×8, an
              // exact box, no font involved) and the pause is two 2px bars from
              // one element's left and right borders. Both boxes are known
              // integers, so flex centring is geometric and identical at every
              // disc size and every deck scale.
              //
              // pointerEvents: none, so a press always lands on the badge that
              // owns the handler rather than on the mark inside it.
              playMark: this.mapSeatOn(st, key(name), name)
                ? { width: '6px', height: '8px', boxSizing: 'border-box',
                    borderLeft: '2px solid var(--gold)', borderRight: '2px solid var(--gold)',
                    pointerEvents: 'none' }
                : { width: 0, height: 0,
                    borderStyle: 'solid', borderWidth: '4px 0 4px 6px',
                    borderColor: 'transparent transparent transparent var(--gold)',
                    pointerEvents: 'none' },
            playClick: (e) => {
              if (e && e.stopPropagation) e.stopPropagation();
              if (pending) return;
              // ⚠ AFTER play(), NOT BEFORE. play() clears mapSeat — that is how a
              // press on any other surface releases the seat — so claiming it
              // first meant the map's own press wiped its own claim. The seat,
              // so the pause lands on the circle that was pressed and not on
              // every circle sharing its record. See mapSeatOn.
              // This seat's own track — see the structured builder's note, and the
              // ask recorded there. `kind` is 'c' for the curator; a guest's
              // position comes from the drop's own guest list.
              this.play(this.recFor(name), kind === 'c' ? 0 : (guests.indexOf(name) + 1));
              this.setState({ mapSeat: key(name) });
            },
                nameStyle: besides
                  ? { fontSize: '11.5px', lineHeight: 1.3, minWidth: 0, color: sel || hov ? 'var(--gold)' : 'var(--ink)', fontStyle: pending ? 'italic' : 'normal', overflow: 'hidden', textOverflow: 'ellipsis', whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }
                  : { fontSize: kind === 'c' ? '11px' : '10.5px', fontWeight: kind === 'c' ? 600 : 400, lineHeight: 1.28, textAlign: 'center', width: '100%', color: sel || hov ? 'var(--gold)' : 'var(--ink)', fontStyle: pending ? 'italic' : 'normal', overflowWrap: 'break-word' },
                enter: () => { if (!pending) this.setState({ mnhover: key(name) }); },
                leave: () => this.setState({ mnhover: null }),
                // ── TOUCH MUST NOT CLOSE WHAT IT JUST OPENED (2026-09-04,
                // interim 5) ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
                // A tap fires a synthetic mouseenter AND a click. The enter
                // opened the bubble and the click's toggle shut it again, so on
                // a phone the dots were unusable while reading correctly under
                // a mouse. Click is open-or-KEEP: it selects, and only a second
                // tap on the ALREADY-selected dot clears it, which is the one
                // unambiguous intent. The target grows to 44px through a
                // transparent pad centred on the dot (tapPad), so the dot's
                // DRAWN size stays the composition's — the hit area is not the
                // mark.
                tapPad: pending ? { display: 'none' } : {
                  position: 'absolute', left: '50%', top: '50%',
                  width: '44px', height: '44px', margin: '-22px 0 0 -22px',
                  borderRadius: '50%', background: 'transparent', zIndex: 1
                },
                click: (e) => {
                  if (e && e.stopPropagation) e.stopPropagation();
                  if (pending) return;
                  if (st.sel === name) { this.setState({ sel: null, selDrop: null, selTrack: null, selCurator: null, selKind: null }); return; }
                  this.setState({ sel: name, selDrop: v.label + ' · Drop ' + d[0], selTrack: d[2], selCurator: cName, selKind: kind });
                }
              };
            };
            const wires = M.dir === 'v'
              ? [
                  { key: 'w1', style: wire(G.CX + M.c / 2, G.CY, G.MIDX - G.CX - M.c / 2, 0, !!onFan) },
                  { key: 'w2', style: wire(G.MIDX, G.IYA + M.i / 2, 0, G.IYB - G.IYA, !!onFan) },
                  { key: 'w3', style: wire(G.MIDX, G.IYA + M.i / 2, G.IX - M.i / 2 - G.MIDX, 0, !!onFan) },
                  { key: 'w4', style: wire(G.MIDX, G.IYB + M.i / 2, G.IX - M.i / 2 - G.MIDX, 0, !!onFan) }
                ]
              : [
                  { key: 'w1', style: wire(G.CX, G.CY + M.c / 2, 0, G.SPINE - G.CY - M.c / 2, !!onFan) },
                  { key: 'w2', style: wire(G.IXA, G.SPINE, G.IXB - G.IXA, 0, !!onFan) },
                  { key: 'w3', style: wire(G.IXA, G.SPINE, 0, G.IYA - G.SPINE, !!onFan) },
                  { key: 'w4', style: wire(G.IXB, G.SPINE, 0, G.IYB - G.SPINE, !!onFan) }
                ];
            return {
              key: 'f' + di,
              drop: 'D' + String(d[0]).padStart(2, '0'),
              dropStyle: {
                position: 'absolute', left: 0, top: 0,
                fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace", fontSize: '8px', letterSpacing: '0.16em',
                textTransform: 'uppercase', color: onFan ? 'var(--gold)' : 'var(--ink40)'
              },
              fanStyle: M.dir === 'v'
                ? { position: 'relative', height: G.H + 'px', marginBottom: '10px' } // was M.tight ? 6 : 10 — dead
                : { position: 'relative', height: G.H + 'px', minWidth: 0 },
              wires: wires,
              people: [person(cName, 'c', null), person(guests[0], 'a', 0), person(guests[1], 'a', 1)]
            };
          })
        });
      }

      return {
        key: 'b' + vi,
        groups: groups,
        label: v.label, year: vi === 0 ? '2025' : '2026',
        note: 'eight drops · twenty-four artists',
        bandStyle: M.dir === 'v'
          ? { marginTop: '20px' }
          : { marginTop: vi === 0 ? '20px' : '26px' },
        gridStyle: M.dir === 'v'
          ? { marginTop: '12px', display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(' + M.colsPerVol + ', minmax(0, 1fr))', columnGap: M.gap + 'px' }
          : { marginTop: '12px' }
      };
    });
  }

  slug(name) {
    const folded = name.normalize ? name.normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '') : name;
    return folded.toLowerCase().replace(/[’'.,()]/g, '').replace(/\s+/g, '-');
  }

  // The artist's STORED handle from INDEX[6], or null. Declared beside slug()
  // so the two are read together: slug() is a transformation and stays exactly
  // as engineering's side has it; this is a supplied fact and outranks it.
  handleFor(name) {
    if (!name) return null;
    if (!this._handles) {
      this._handles = {};
      (this.data('INDEX') || []).forEach(r => { if (r && r[0] && r[6]) this._handles[r[0]] = String(r[6]); });
    }
    return this._handles[name] || null;
  }

  initials(name) {
    return name.split(/[\s·]+/).filter(w => /[A-Za-z]/.test(w)).slice(0, 2).map(w => w[0].toUpperCase()).join('');
  }

  // Every name that appears as an invited artist AND as a curator, so the tile
  // can say so — that crossing is the whole point of the series.
  laterCurator(name) {
    if (this._lc) return !!this._lc[name];
    this._lc = {};
    this.data('VOLS').forEach(v => v.drops.forEach(d => { this._lc[d[1]] = 1; }));
    return !!this._lc[name];
  }

  // The control's OWN left edge, in stage-local pixels, clamped so a panel of
  // width w still fits the stage. anchor() below right-aligns to the control,
  // which is what a ⋮ menu wants; a panel that hangs UNDER a button wants its
  // left edge instead (2026-09-09).
  anchorLeft(e, w) {
    const el = e && e.currentTarget;
    const stage = el && el.closest ? el.closest('[data-theme]') : null;
    if (!el || !stage) return null;
    const sb = stage.getBoundingClientRect();
    const tb = el.getBoundingClientRect();
    const scale = sb.width / (stage.offsetWidth || sb.width) || 1;
    const W = stage.offsetWidth;
    const tx = (tb.left - sb.left) / scale;
    return Math.round(Math.max(8, Math.min(W - w - 8, tx)));
  }

  // Anchor an overlay to the control that opened it, in stage-local pixels.
  anchor(e, w, h) {
    const el = e && e.currentTarget;
    const stage = el && el.closest ? el.closest('[data-theme]') : null;
    let left = 8, top = 8;
    if (el && stage) {
      const sb = stage.getBoundingClientRect();
      const tb = el.getBoundingClientRect();
      const scale = sb.width / (stage.offsetWidth || sb.width) || 1;
      const W = stage.offsetWidth, H = stage.offsetHeight;
      const tx = (tb.left - sb.left) / scale;
      const ty = (tb.top - sb.top) / scale;
      const th = tb.height / scale, tw = tb.width / scale;
      const up = ty - h - 8 >= 8;
      top = Math.max(8, Math.min(H - h - 8, up ? ty - h - 8 : ty + th + 8));
      left = Math.max(8, Math.min(W - w - 8, tx + tw - w));
    }
    return { left: Math.round(left), top: Math.round(top) };
  }

  // The buy sheet — the one path a record shop cannot leave closed. Physical
  // first (LP, then CS), download last; a sold-out format keeps its row, struck
  // through, and offers a notify instead of a dead button.
  // [format, variant, price, state]  state: '' | 'few' | 'sold'
