    ['DL', 'FLAC, ALAC, MP3 and more', '$9.00', '']
  ];

  /* ══ DC-DEFAULTS:END run=25 drop=REC_NOTES,REC_COVERS,CART,BUY keep=FILTER_COUNT ══ */
  openBuy(rec) {
    return (e) => {
      if (e && e.stopPropagation) e.stopPropagation();
      const pos = this.anchor(e, 340, 336);
      this.setState({ buy: { left: pos.left, top: pos.top, rec: rec == null ? null : rec, pick: 0 }, ctx: null, heroMenu: false, browseOpen: false, explore: false, cart: false });
    };
  }

  openCtx(kind, rec) {
    return (e) => {
      if (e && e.stopPropagation) e.stopPropagation();
      const el = e && e.currentTarget;
      const stage = el && el.closest ? el.closest('[data-theme]') : null;
      const rows = this.ctxRowsFor(kind);
      const h = this.ctxHeight(rows);
      const w = this.CTX_W;
      let left = 8, top = 8;
      if (el && stage) {
        const sb = stage.getBoundingClientRect();
        const tb = el.getBoundingClientRect();
        const scale = sb.width / (stage.offsetWidth || sb.width) || 1;
        const W = stage.offsetWidth, H = stage.offsetHeight;
        // Stage-local coordinates of the trigger.
        const tx = (tb.left - sb.left) / scale;
        const ty = (tb.top - sb.top) / scale;
        const th = tb.height / scale;
        const tw = tb.width / scale;
        // Right edge of the control anchors the menu's right edge; open upward
        // when there is room, downward when there is not, clamped either way.
        const up = ty - h - 8 >= 8;
        top = up ? ty - h - 8 : ty + th + 8;
        top = Math.max(8, Math.min(H - h - 8, top));
        left = Math.max(8, Math.min(W - w - 8, tx + tw - w));
      }
      this.setState({
        ctx: { kind: kind, rec: rec == null ? null : rec, left: Math.round(left), top: Math.round(top) },
        ctxHover: null, heroMenu: false, browseOpen: false, explore: false, cart: false, menuOpen: false, albumMenu: false, trackMenu: null
      });
    };
  }

  // One expansion, used by BOTH the positioner and the renderer so the height
  // it measures is the height it draws.
  // PROTOCOL — social links are CONDITIONAL, never placeholders. A menu shows
  // "Follow the artist" only when that artist actually has links on file; with
  // nothing on file the whole section drops (heading, rows and its rule) rather
  // than offering dead links. SOCIALS holds them per artist name:
  //   SOCIALS['Jeremy Cunningham'] = { instagram: 'https://…', site: 'https://…' }
  // Add an entry and that artist's Follow section appears with only the links
  // present. Nothing is on file yet, so the section is currently omitted
  // everywhere and a Buy row takes the space instead — buying matters more here
  // than following.
  // Keyed by artist name → { instagram, site, spotify, apple, bandcamp, youtube }.
  // Two mock artists so the tool shows the row; the site supplies the rest.
  /* ══ DC-DEFAULTS:START run=26 drop=SOCIALS ══ */
  DEFAULT_SOCIALS = {
    'Jeremy Cunningham': { instagram: 'https://instagram.com/', bandcamp: 'https://bandcamp.com/', spotify: 'https://open.spotify.com/', youtube: 'https://youtube.com/' },
    'Balmorhea': { instagram: 'https://instagram.com/', bandcamp: 'https://bandcamp.com/', site: 'https://balmorhea.com/' }
  };

  /* ══ DC-DEFAULTS:END run=26 drop=SOCIALS ══ */
  // THE NEWEST RELEASED DROP — one place, because array position is not release
  // status. Volume Two's drops 6 to 8 exist in VOLS but are unreleased and have
  // no covers, so anything that says "latest" has to stop at this anchor rather
  // than take the last entry. Update this on a release and the drops card, the
  // journal sidebar and its featured artists all move together.
  /* ══ DC-DEFAULTS:START run=27 keep=NEWEST_RELEASED ══ */
  DEFAULT_NEWEST_RELEASED = { vol: 2, drop: 5 };

  /* ══ DC-DEFAULTS:END run=27 keep=NEWEST_RELEASED ══ */
  newestReleased() {
    const a = this.data('NEWEST_RELEASED');
    const vol = (a && a.vol > 0) ? this.data('VOLS')[a.vol - 1] : null;
    const row = vol && vol.drops ? vol.drops[a.drop - 1] : null;
    return {
      label: (a && a.vol > 0) ? ('Vol. ' + a.vol + ' Drop ' + a.drop) : '',
      curator: row ? row[1] : '',
      guests: row && row[3] ? row[3] : []
    };
  }

  hasSocials(name) {
    const s = name ? this.data('SOCIALS')[name] : null;
    return !!(s && (s.instagram || s.site));
  }

  ctxRowsFor(kind, who) {
    const rows = this.CTX[kind] || [];
    const keepFollow = this.hasSocials(who || this.state.ctxArtist);
    const out = [];
    let skipping = false;
    rows.forEach(r => {
      const head = String(r[0]).indexOf('§') === 0;
      // Drop the Follow section: its heading, every row under it, and the rule
      // that preceded it (already pushed, so it is popped back off).
      if (head && /Follow the artist/i.test(String(r[1] || r[0]))) {
        if (!keepFollow) {
          skipping = true;
          if (out.length && out[out.length - 1][0] === '--') out.pop();
          return;
        }
        skipping = false;
      } else if (head || r[0] === '--') {
        if (skipping && r[0] === '--') { skipping = false; return; }
        skipping = false;
      }
      if (skipping) return;
      // Map rows do not exist on a phone (2026-09-14) — see CTX's artistMap.
      if (r[3] === 'artistMap' && this.phoneNoMap()) {
        if (out.length && out[out.length - 1][0] === '--') out.pop();
        return;
      }
      if (r[0] !== '$$') { out.push(r); return; }
      this.data('BUY').forEach((b, i) => {
        const sold = b[3] === 'sold';
        // The short variant only — the sheet carries the full description.
        // Anything longer truncates inside the 300px menu.
        const variant = b[0] === 'DL' ? 'Download' : String(b[1]).split(' · ')[0];
        // Every row carries a glyph. One per format, in the same weight as the
        // menu's other icons: a disc for vinyl, a shell for tape, an arrow for
        // the download. ◎ is not reused here — it means "a service" further
        // down the menu.
        //
        // THE GLYPH NAMES THE PHYSICAL OBJECT (DQ-37, 2026-09-01). The build
        // resolves real Shopify variants to a FAMILY and maps the family, since
        // live variants carry no LP/CS code. Flexi discs are VINYL — a flexi is
        // a record you put on a turntable — so 'Flexi Disc + Digital' is ◉, not
        // ↓. General rule for bundles: a row that pairs a physical format with a
        // download takes the PHYSICAL half's glyph, and the row's own text
        // carries the bundle. Only a digital-only row is ↓.
        const glyph = b[0] === 'LP' ? '◉' : b[0] === 'CS' ? '▭' : '↓';
        out.push([glyph, b[0] + ' · ' + variant, sold ? 'Sold out' : b[2], sold ? '' : 'buy:' + i]);
      });
    });
    // A trailing rule left by a dropped section.
    while (out.length && out[out.length - 1][0] === '--') out.pop();
    return out;
  }

  // ── res() · THE ONE PLACE AN ASSET PATH BECOMES A URL ────────────────────
  // Published, this file is bundled into a single self-contained document and
  // its images become blob URLs held on window.__resources, keyed by the ids
  // declared in the helmet's ext-resource-dependency metas. Unpublished, that
  // object does not exist and the path is already correct. res() resolves the
  // first and falls back to the second, so ONE code path serves both and there
  // is no published-only branch to rot.
  //
  // The id rule is mechanical, so it can never drift from the metas: drop the
  // top folder (public/ brand/ assets/), drop the extension, collapse anything
  // that is not a letter or a digit to a hyphen, lowercase.
  //
  // Remote URLs pass through untouched — the four f4.bcbits.com covers are
  // Bandcamp's and are documented as remote; a strict host cannot fetch them
  // and they are not ours to inline.
  // ── THE GROUP PAGE'S VALUES ───────────────────────────────────────────────
  // Was the renderVals() of Stellar Atlas Group.dc.html. Kept as its own method
  // rather than folded into renderVals() so the group's ~60 keys stay legible as
  // a set, and exposed under one `grp` key so nothing can collide.
  groupVals(W, st) {
    // W and the theme arrive from the site's renderVals — the group is a ROUTE
    // now, not a separate document, so it must not keep a second viewport or a
    // second theme. Its own resize listener and `theme` prop are gone with the
    // merge (2026-09-02); the picker drives both.
    const p = this.props;
    const w = W;
    const mid = w < 1000;
    const narrow = w < 720;
    const tiny = w < 520;
    // Five label cards only hold their copy at five full-width tracks.
    const labelCards = w >= 1000;
    const svcCols = tiny ? 1 : narrow ? 2 : mid ? 2 : 4;
    const lblCols = tiny ? 1 : narrow ? 2 : mid ? 2 : 3;
    const light = st.theme === 'light';
    // The Atlas mark exists only as black ink on transparent; no knockout variant
    // exists in the codebase, so a light one was generated from it.
    const atlas = light ? 'brand/atlas-map-mark.png' : 'brand/atlas-map-mark-white.png';
    const atlasBg = (px, op) => ({
      width: px + 'px', height: px + 'px', flexShrink: 0,
      background: 'url(' + this.res(atlas) + ') center/contain no-repeat', opacity: op
    });

    // EVERY GRID HERE DIVIDES EVENLY BY ITS OWN COUNT. Four products in four
    // columns, five labels in five, and the seven services are pills that do not
    // grow — so no row can end short and no cell can be left empty. That, not the
    // card idea, was what produced the grey gaps in the first pass.
    // EVERY CARD THE SAME WIDTH. Nothing spans: a double-width last card gave
    // that one description ~100 characters per line against ~51 in the others,
    // which read as two odd slabs among twelve even cards. Column counts are
    // chosen to leave the smallest possible tail instead — 7 over 4, 5 over 3 —
    // and because each card carries its own border rather than the grid painting
    // one behind it, an unused cell at the end is plain empty space, not a block.
    // AN ARROW, NOT A URL. Anything with a live site gets a ↗ in its top-right
    // corner; the address itself is never printed on the page.
    // Explore-menu marks are MASKED and filled with --ink rather than drawn as
    // background images, so one asset works in both themes. Same helper the site
    // file uses for its own menu.
    // A WIDER SLOT THAN IT IS TALL. Every asset is now cropped tight to its own
    // ink, so `contain` fills the slot: square marks reach the full 44px height,
    // and NNA — whose mark is 2.34 times wider than it is tall — gets 64px of
    // width to work with instead of being squeezed to 18px tall in a square box.
    // The slot is the same for every row, so the names still share a left edge.
    const exMark = (url, size) => ({
      width: '64px', height: '44px', flexShrink: 0, backgroundColor: 'var(--ink)',
      WebkitMaskImage: 'url(' + this.res(url) + ')', maskImage: 'url(' + this.res(url) + ')',
      WebkitMaskRepeat: 'no-repeat', maskRepeat: 'no-repeat',
      WebkitMaskPosition: 'center', maskPosition: 'center',
      WebkitMaskSize: size, maskSize: size
    });

    // The card's outbound arrow. 24px drawn, 44px HIT on a phone (2026-09-07,
    // audit): eight of these sit on the group page and every one was 26²
    // against the 44 minimum. The drawn box stays 24 — it is a corner mark on a
    // card, not a button — and the target grows by padding the box out and
    // pulling the same amount back off its offsets, so the mark does not move a
    // pixel. Same intent as the map dots' transparent pad, expressed on an
    // element that already has a box.
    const linkArrow = (href) => href
      ? { position: 'absolute', top: (narrow ? -1 : 14) + 'px', right: (narrow ? -1 : 14) + 'px',
          display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center',
          justifyContent: 'center', width: '24px', height: '24px', borderRadius: '4px',
          padding: narrow ? '10px' : 0, boxSizing: 'content-box',
          border: '1px solid var(--hair)', fontSize: '12px', lineHeight: 1,
          color: 'var(--gold)', textDecoration: 'none' }
      : { display: 'none' };

    const card = {
      position: 'relative',
      display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', minWidth: 0, boxSizing: 'border-box',
      padding: tiny ? '20px 18px' : '20px 16px',
      borderRadius: '6px', border: '1px solid var(--hair)'
    };

    return {
      heroLine: p.groupHeroLine ?? 'An independent music universe',
      // THE ATLAS MARK HAS A SIZE FLOOR. It is an intricate radial star map of
      // fine concentric arcs; below roughly 80px the arcs merge into a grey fan
      // with no silhouette, and no amount of resolution fixes detail density. So
      // it appears exactly once, at 250px in the hero. Header, section-03 head and
      // footer set the name in type instead. 58px in the head was the same
      // scribble this comment describes, and section 03 needs no badge anyway —
      // it is Stellar Atlas's own section on Stellar Atlas's own page. The repo's
      // small "star-map-*" files are decorative ornaments, not logo variants, and
      // must not stand in for the company mark.
      markStyleLg: Object.assign(atlasBg(narrow ? 140 : mid ? 200 : 250, 0.95), { justifySelf: 'end' }),

      headerStyle: {
        // ⚠ NO GUIDE LIFT HERE. I put one on this branch first, and it is inert:
        // this is groupVals()'s header, and the group page has no chrome header
        // and no Guide (chromeHeader is false there), so the orientation can
        // never be up while this style is read. The live lift is guideLift on
        // renderVals' own headerStyle chain — five branches, and the landing
        // uses one of them.
        position: 'sticky', top: 0, zIndex: 20, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center',
        height: narrow ? '62px' : '72px', padding: '0 ' + (narrow ? 22 : 40) + 'px', gap: '20px',
        boxSizing: 'border-box', background: 'var(--bg)', borderBottom: '1px solid var(--hair)'
      },
      navStyle: (tiny || narrow)
        ? { display: 'none' }
        : { display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: narrow ? '16px' : '26px', flexShrink: 0,
            fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace", fontSize: '9px', letterSpacing: '0.16em',
            textTransform: 'uppercase' },

      heroStyle: narrow
        ? { display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column-reverse', alignItems: 'flex-start', gap: '26px', padding: '38px 0 34px' }
        : { display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'minmax(0, 1fr) auto', gap: mid ? '36px' : '56px',
            alignItems: 'center', padding: (mid ? '52px' : '68px') + ' 0 ' + (mid ? '44px' : '56px') },
      // The user's three lines, verbatim and in their order: name, then
      // "An independent music universe", then the paragraph that completes it.
      h2Style: {
        marginTop: '14px', fontFamily: 'Archivo, Helvetica, sans-serif',
        fontSize: (narrow ? 20 : mid ? 23 : 26) + 'px',
        fontWeight: 400, letterSpacing: '-0.015em', lineHeight: 1.24,
        color: 'var(--gold)', textWrap: 'pretty'
      },
      h1Style: {
        fontFamily: 'Archivo, Helvetica, sans-serif',
        fontSize: (narrow ? 36 : mid ? 46 : 56) + 'px',
        fontWeight: 600, letterSpacing: '-0.032em', lineHeight: 1.03, color: 'var(--ink)', textWrap: 'pretty'
      },
      leadStyle: {
        marginTop: '20px', maxWidth: '56ch',
        fontSize: (narrow ? 14.5 : 15.5) + 'px', lineHeight: 1.68, color: 'var(--ink70)', textWrap: 'pretty'
      },

      statStyle: {
        display: 'grid',
        gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(' + (tiny ? 1 : narrow ? 1 : 3) + ', minmax(0, 1fr))',
        gap: narrow ? '26px' : '40px',
        padding: (narrow ? '26px' : '30px') + ' 0',
        borderTop: '1px solid var(--hair)', borderBottom: '1px solid var(--hair)'
      },
      // The three companies in the page's own order, each with its count and the
      // one line that says what it is for. Client's wording.
      stats: [
        { key: 's1', n: '5', k: 'Record labels', who: 'Stellar Atlas Label Group', line: 'The music we believe in.', href: '#labels' },
        { key: 's2', n: '7', k: 'Service offerings', who: 'Clandestine Label Services', line: 'The team and expertise behind the releases.', href: '#services' },
        { key: 's3', n: '4', k: 'Productivity suites', who: 'Haulix Software Studio', line: 'The tools behind the work.', href: '#software' }
      ].map(s => Object.assign(s, {
        // Each stat is the jump to its own section — the same anchors the header
        // nav uses, offered where the reader is actually reading.
        box: {
          display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', alignItems: 'flex-start', minWidth: 0,
          padding: '16px 18px 18px', margin: '-16px -18px -18px', boxSizing: 'border-box',
          borderRadius: '7px', border: '1px solid transparent',
          textDecoration: 'none', cursor: 'pointer',
          transition: 'border-color 160ms ease, background 160ms ease'
        }
      })),

      secStyle: { padding: (narrow ? 44 : mid ? 56 : 68) + 'px 0 0', scrollMarginTop: '86px' },
      // The lede sits UNDER its company, not in a second column beside it — as a
      // side column it read as floating loose in the middle of the page.
      secHeadStyle: { display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '13px', paddingBottom: narrow ? '22px' : '26px' },
      secLedeStyle: { minWidth: 0, maxWidth: '62ch', fontSize: '13.5px', lineHeight: 1.65, color: 'var(--ink70)', textWrap: 'pretty' },

      prodGridStyle: {
        display: 'grid',
        gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(' + (tiny ? 1 : narrow ? 2 : mid ? 2 : 4) + ', minmax(0, 1fr))',
        gap: '8px'
      },
      // FIVE divides evenly by five and by nothing else useful, so there is no
      // honest 2- or 3-column fallback: two columns leaves a lone card and half
      // an empty row. Below 1000 the cards become rows instead — mark and name
      // left, domain pinned right — which fills the width by design and gives the
      // domain strings the room they need (at five tracks they had 118px and
      // needed 138px, so they painted straight through the card border).
      labelGridStyle: {
        display: 'grid',
        gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(' + lblCols + ', minmax(0, 1fr))',
        gap: '8px'
      },

      contactWrapStyle: { padding: (narrow ? 48 : 72) + 'px 0 ' + (narrow ? 48 : 72) + 'px' },
      contactBoxStyle: {
        display: 'flex', flexDirection: narrow ? 'column' : 'row',
        alignItems: narrow ? 'flex-start' : 'center', gap: narrow ? '20px' : '40px',
        padding: narrow ? '28px 24px' : '34px 36px', boxSizing: 'border-box',
        borderRadius: '8px', border: '1px solid var(--goldsoft)', background: 'var(--tile)'
      },
      contactHeadStyle: {
        marginTop: '12px', fontFamily: 'Archivo, Helvetica, sans-serif',
        fontSize: (narrow ? 20 : 25) + 'px', fontWeight: 600, letterSpacing: '-0.018em',
        lineHeight: 1.2, color: 'var(--ink)', textWrap: 'pretty'
      },

      footerStyle: {
        borderTop: '1px solid var(--hair)',
        padding: (narrow ? '26px 22px' : '30px 40px'), boxSizing: 'border-box'
      },
      // An index of the companies, not a repeat of the header nav.
      footerCompanies: this.data('GRP_LABELS').map(l => l[0]).concat(['Clandestine', 'Haulix'])
        .map((n, i) => ({ key: 'fc' + i, name: n })),

      products: this.data('GRP_PRODUCTS').map((x, i) => ({
        key: 'pr' + i, name: x[0], cat: x[2], desc: x[4], tag: x[1],
        // §1a · the two BETA products have no address yet, so they get no href
        // rather than href="". Their ↗ still draws; it just is not a link until
        // there is somewhere for it to go.
        href: x[3] ? 'https://' + x[3] : null,
        linkStyle: linkArrow(x[3]),
        box: card,
        badge: {
          display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', alignSelf: 'flex-start', height: '18px',
          padding: '0 7px', boxSizing: 'border-box', borderRadius: '3px', flexShrink: 0,
          borderWidth: '1px', borderStyle: 'solid',
          borderColor: 'var(--goldsoft)',
          fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace", fontSize: '8px', letterSpacing: '0.14em',
          textTransform: 'uppercase', color: 'var(--gold)'
        }
      })),
      // THE SHARED EXPLORE MENU. Same structure and hover behaviour as the label
      // sites' — Group, Labels, Services, Software — so one control moves between
      // every site in the family. Here the rows are real links to each site,
      // because from the parent there is no in-app label to switch to.
      // ONE EXPLORE STATE, THE SITE'S (2026-09-02). The group used to keep its
      // own `explore` flag; merged in, that would have been a second menu state
      // on the same page, and the two could disagree.
      exploreOpen: !!st.explore,
      openExplore: () => this.setState({ explore: true }),
      closeExplore: () => this.setState({ explore: false }),
      toggleExplore: () => this.setState({ explore: !st.explore }),
      // ── THE WRAPPER HUGS THE BUTTON (2026-09-06, Tom) ─────────────────────
      // This was `display: block`, so its height was the LINE BOX — the div's
      // own font strut plus the button's inline-flex box — rather than the
      // button. The panel anchors at top: 100%, so it dropped from a taller box
      // than Clandestine's and opened noticeably lower on the group header,
      // which is the report.
      //
      // inline-flex makes the wrapper exactly the button's height, which is
      // what Clandestine's wrappers already are — so both headers now drop
      // their panel from the same thing. Same lesson as the two Clandestine
      // menus: `top: 100%` is only as consistent as the boxes it measures.
      exploreWrapStyle: { position: 'relative', flexShrink: 0, display: tiny ? 'none' : 'inline-flex', alignItems: 'center' },
      exploreBtnStyle: {
        display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', height: '32px', padding: '0 10px',
        boxSizing: 'border-box', borderRadius: '5px', cursor: 'pointer', whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
        border: '1px solid ' + (st.explore ? 'var(--goldsoft)' : 'transparent'),
        background: st.explore ? 'rgba(219,191,61,0.1)' : 'transparent',
        fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace", fontSize: '9px', letterSpacing: '0.16em',
        textTransform: 'uppercase', color: st.explore ? 'var(--gold)' : 'var(--ink70)'
      },
      // 372px is what the longest NAME needs — Clandestine Label Services. No
      // scroll: opening the services grows the panel. Domains are gone from the
      // rows; a switcher needs to say which company, not which URL.
      // Masked and filled with --ink, like the site's Explore rows, so the menu
      // survives the light theme instead of going blank.
      groupMarkStyle: exMark('brand/marks/stellar-atlas.png', 'contain'),
      clandestineHeadMarkStyle: {
        width: '48px', height: '48px', flexShrink: 0, backgroundColor: 'var(--ink)',
        WebkitMaskImage: 'url(' + this.res('brand/marks/clandestine.svg') + ')',
        maskImage: 'url(' + this.res('brand/marks/clandestine.svg') + ')',
        WebkitMaskRepeat: 'no-repeat', maskRepeat: 'no-repeat',
        WebkitMaskPosition: 'left center', maskPosition: 'left center',
        WebkitMaskSize: 'contain', maskSize: 'contain'
      },
      explorePanelStyle: {
        // FLUSH WITH THE BUTTON. At top:40px there was an 8px dead band between
        // the button and the panel; crossing it left the hover wrapper and shut
        // the menu before the pointer arrived. The panel starts at the button's
        // bottom edge and pads inside instead.
        //
        // ⚠ top: 32px WAS THE LABEL HEADER'S BUTTON HEIGHT (2026-09-06, Tom).
        // A magic number measured on one header: Clandestine's controls are
        // taller (44px, its own bar), so on those pages the panel opened ~40px
        // below the bar with a visible band of page between the two — the
        // screenshot. `top: 100%` is the same intent expressed as a relation:
        // the panel's top IS its wrapper's bottom, whatever that wrapper is,
        // so it is flush on every header and cannot drift when one changes.
        position: 'absolute', top: '100%', right: 0, zIndex: 40, marginTop: '10px',
        // ⚠ 10px is the SAME drop the Clandestine panels use (CLS_PANEL_ANCHOR).
        // Every header panel in the file falls the same distance from its
        // button; if one of them ever looks different again, the cause is the
        // WRAPPER's box and not this number — see exploreWrapStyle's note,
        // where a display: block wrapper had been measuring its line box.
        width: '372px',
        padding: '18px 0 8px', boxSizing: 'border-box', borderRadius: '7px',
        border: '1px solid var(--hair20)', background: 'var(--bg)',
        boxShadow: '0 22px 60px rgba(0,0,0,0.5)',
        display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column'
      },
      exploreSectionStyle: {
        padding: '8px 20px 6px', fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace", fontSize: '9px',
        fontWeight: 600, letterSpacing: '0.28em', textTransform: 'uppercase', color: 'var(--ink40)'
      },
      exploreNameStyle: { fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace", fontSize: '12px', fontWeight: 600, letterSpacing: '0.12em', textTransform: 'uppercase', whiteSpace: 'nowrap', color: 'var(--ink)' },
      exploreSubStyle: {
        fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace", fontSize: '9px', letterSpacing: '0.18em',
        textTransform: 'uppercase', color: 'var(--ink55)'
      },
      exploreRowStyle: {
        display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '14px', padding: '10px 20px',
        boxSizing: 'border-box', cursor: 'pointer', textDecoration: 'none'
      },
      exploreHereStyle: {
        display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '14px', padding: '10px 20px',
        boxSizing: 'border-box', background: 'rgba(219,191,61,0.08)'
      },
      // NO EXPANDER. Seven service rows opening inside the switcher made it messy
      // and long; one line saying what Clandestine actually does carries the same
      // information, and the row itself goes to their site.
      clandestineRowStyle: {
        display: 'flex', alignItems: 'flex-start', gap: '14px', padding: '10px 20px 12px',
        boxSizing: 'border-box', cursor: 'pointer', textDecoration: 'none'
      },
      clandestineMarkStyle: exMark('brand/marks/clandestine.svg', 'contain'),
      // Haulix is a filled badge rather than line art, so it fills its slot as a
      // circle instead of being scaled to match the drawn marks' ink.
      // Section 03's head mark. It was a raw url() in the markup, which cannot
      // route through res() — so published it would have been the one blank
      // graphic on the page. Same class as the perk-tile regression.
      haulixHeadMarkStyle: {
        width: '44px', height: '44px', flexShrink: 0, borderRadius: '50%',
        background: 'url(' + this.res('public/brand/software/haulix-mark.png') + ') center/cover no-repeat'
      },
      haulixMarkStyle: exMark('public/brand/software/haulix-mark.png', 'contain'),
      // ── ONE HOME FOR THE MAP (2026-09-05) ─────────────────────────────────
      // These read 'https://' + GRP_LABELS[i][1] — a SECOND copy of each
      // address, three of which were the stale values the URL map had just
      // corrected elsewhere. siteUrl() is the map; index 4 is ATH.
      // ── A FOURTH DOMAIN VARIANT (2026-09-05) ──────────────────────────────
      // The group's two contact links were mailto:info@stellaratlasmusicgroup.com
      // — matching neither StellarAtlasLabelGroup.com from the URL map nor
      // anything in SITE_URLS. Per the r4 ruling that a half-written address is
      // worse than none, the address is now DERIVED from the map's own domain,
      // so there is one group domain in the file and the mailto cannot drift
      // from it. ABOUT_CONTACTS still overrides where the site supplies real
      // per-label addresses.
      // ⚠ NOT ABOUT_CONTACTS. My first version read the general row from that
      // table, which pulled in its `info@example.com` mock — a placeholder
      // address on a live contact link, the exact fault the r4 ruling was about
      // (a half-written address renders as a broken site). The group's address
      // is DERIVED from the map's own domain instead, so there is one group
      // domain in the file and the mailto cannot drift from it. GRP_CONTACT
      // overrides when the site supplies the real one.
      grpMailAddress: (this.data('GRP_CONTACT') || '') ||
        ('info@' + String(this.siteUrl('group')).replace(/^https?:\/\//, '').toLowerCase()),
      grpMailto: 'mailto:' + ((this.data('GRP_CONTACT') || '') ||
        ('info@' + String(this.siteUrl('group')).replace(/^https?:\/\//, '').toLowerCase())),
      grpMailLabel: (this.data('GRP_CONTACT') || '') ||
        ('info@' + String(this.siteUrl('group')).replace(/^https?:\/\//, '').toLowerCase()),
      exploreLabels: this.data('GRP_LABELS').map((l, i) => ({
        key: 'ex' + i, name: l[0],
        href: this.siteUrl(i === 4 ? 'ath' : i),
        box: { display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '14px', padding: '10px 20px', boxSizing: 'border-box', cursor: 'pointer', textDecoration: 'none' },
        // A FIXED SQUARE SLOT, no per-mark multiplier: in a row the mark is a left
        // column, and a wider box pushed NNA's name out of the shared left edge.
        mark: exMark(l[2], 'contain')
      })),
      customName: this.data('GRP_CUSTOM')[0],
      customTag: this.data('GRP_CUSTOM')[1],
      customDesc: this.data('GRP_CUSTOM')[2],
      customRowStyle: {
        display: 'flex', flexDirection: narrow ? 'column' : 'row',
        alignItems: narrow ? 'flex-start' : 'center', gap: narrow ? '10px' : '18px',
        marginTop: '8px', padding: narrow ? '18px' : '18px 20px', boxSizing: 'border-box',
        borderRadius: '6px', border: '1px dashed var(--hair20)'
      },
      customBadgeStyle: {
        display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', height: '18px', padding: '0 7px', boxSizing: 'border-box',
        borderRadius: '3px', flexShrink: 0, border: '1px solid var(--goldsoft)',
        fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace", fontSize: '8px', letterSpacing: '0.14em',
        textTransform: 'uppercase', color: 'var(--gold)'
      },
      svcGridStyle: {
        display: 'grid',
        gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(' + svcCols + ', minmax(0, 1fr))',
        gap: '8px'
      },
      services: this.data('GRP_SERVICES').map((x, i) => ({
        key: 'sv' + i, name: x[0], cat: x[1], desc: x[2], box: card
      })),
      labels: this.data('GRP_LABELS').map((l, i) => {
        return {
          key: 'lb' + i, name: l[0], cat: l[4], desc: l[5],
          // The map, not the column — see exploreLabels' note. The group page
          // is the one surface built entirely for picking a label site, so it
          // was the worst place to keep a private copy of the addresses.
          href: this.siteUrl(i === 4 ? 'ath' : i),
          linkStyle: linkArrow(l[1]),
          box: card,
          // Card marks: masked and filled with --ink, same as the menu, and the
          // same wider-than-tall slot so all five read at one optical size.
          mark: {
            display: 'block', width: '64px', height: '44px', flexShrink: 0,
            backgroundColor: 'var(--ink)',
            WebkitMaskImage: 'url(' + this.res(l[2]) + ')', maskImage: 'url(' + this.res(l[2]) + ')',
            WebkitMaskRepeat: 'no-repeat', maskRepeat: 'no-repeat',
            WebkitMaskPosition: 'left center', maskPosition: 'left center',
            WebkitMaskSize: 'contain', maskSize: 'contain'
          }
        };
      })
    };
  }

  // ── WHICH COLUMN A PAGE BECOMES ─────────────────────────────────────────
  // Read by goStarFn (Grid → Map). Its inverse lives in goArtistsFn, which
  // turns the column back into a page. Only routes whose column shows the SAME
  // content are listed — an artist's page has no column of its own, so it pairs
  // with the artists list, the nearest thing that still holds what you were
  // reading. ATH is not here: its column is the landing deck, handled first.
  MAP_VIEW_FOR = {
    catalogue: 'releases',
    landing: 'releases',
    artists: 'artists',
    artist: 'artists',
    record: 'album',
    radio: 'radio',
    tour: 'tour',
    news: 'news',
    about: 'about'
  };

  res(p) {
    if (!p || typeof p !== 'string') return p;
    if (p.indexOf('http') === 0 || p.indexOf('data:') === 0 || p.indexOf('blob:') === 0) return p;
    const id = p.replace(/^(public|brand|assets)\//, '').replace(/\.[a-z]+$/, '').replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/gi, '-').toLowerCase();
    const R = typeof window !== 'undefined' && window.__resources;
    return (R && R[id]) || p;
  }

  // '' when the drop has no portrait for this name. Never a remote fallback.
  photoFor(name) {
    if (!name) return '';
    const s = this.slug(name);
    if (this.data('PHOTO_HAVE').indexOf(s) === -1) return '';
    return this.res(this.data('PHOTO_BASE') + s + '.jpg');
  }

  ctxHeight(rows) {
    let h = 12;
    rows.forEach(r => { h += r[0] === '--' ? 11 : (String(r[0]).indexOf('§') === 0 ? 26 : 40); });
    return h;
  }

  rowBg(a) { return a === 2 ? 'rgba(219,191,61,0.18)' : a === 1 ? 'rgba(219,191,61,0.14)' : 'transparent'; }
  rowBar(a) { return a === 2 ? 'inset 3px 0 0 0 rgba(219,191,61,0.9)' : a === 1 ? 'inset 3px 0 0 0 rgba(219,191,61,0.6)' : 'none'; }

  menuRow(label, ext) {
    return {
      key: label, label: label, ext: ext || '',
      box: { display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px', height: '40px', padding: '0 10px', borderRadius: '4px', fontSize: '12.5px', color: 'var(--ink70)', cursor: 'pointer' },
      extStyle: { fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace", fontSize: '9px', color: 'var(--ink40)' }
    };
  }

  // Name, domain, mark and scale are grounded in the site file's exploreLabels.
  // The codebase's own normalised switcher marks: each trimmed of its transparent
  // padding and resampled into a common slot. Trimming equalises PADDING, not
  // ink — coverage runs from 6% (NNA) to 25% (ATH). The fourth field is a SLOT
  // WIDTH multiplier, not a background scale: a background-size over 100% is
  // clipped by the element box, which sliced 5px off each side of NNA's zigzag.
  // Every mark stays at `contain` inside its own box, and the wide/short marks
  // simply get a wider box. NNA's ink bbox is 132×56, so it needs the most.
  // MARK PARITY IS IN THE ASSETS, NOT THE CSS. Each mark is cropped to its own
  // ink, scaled so every one shares the same geometric mean (240px on a 384px
  // square), centred, and recoloured to --ink — rebuilt from the 4000px
  // originals. So a wide mark is wider and shorter and a tall one taller and
  // narrower, but all six read the same size in the same square slot. The old
  // per-mark multipliers and background-size percentages are gone: they were
  // compensating in CSS for assets that disagreed, and a wide slot pushed NNA's
  // name out of the Explore panel's left edge.
  // ── THE DOMAIN COLUMN IS THE URL MAP'S, NOT ITS OWN COPY (2026-09-05) ────
  // ⚠ These five carried a SECOND copy of each site's address, and three of
  // them were the exact stale values the URL map corrected in exploreLabels —
  // northernspyrecords.com, nnatapes.com, acrossthehorizon.info. So the pass
  // that "caught" them fixed the Explore panel and left the GROUP PAGE, which
  // is the one surface built entirely for picking a label site. Highest-traffic
  // instance of the thing Tom's ruling forbids.
  //
  // The session's recurring defect, again: two sources for one fact. The column
  // is now written from SITE_URLS (see grpLabels, which reads siteUrl by index)
  // and these literals are the map's values only so the table reads honestly on
  // its own — nothing renders them.
  // The group's public contact address. Empty → derived from the URL map's own
  // group domain, so it can never be a different domain from the site it is on
  // (2026-09-05).
  /* ══ DC-DEFAULTS:START run=28 keep=GRP_CONTACT,GRP_LABELS,GRP_SERVICES,GRP_PRODUCTS,GRP_CUSTOM ══ */
  DEFAULT_GRP_CONTACT = '';
  DEFAULT_GRP_LABELS = [
    ['Northern Spy Records', 'northernspy.music', 'brand/marks/northern-spy.svg', 1, 'Independent / Experimental',
      'Founded in Brooklyn in 2010, Northern Spy releases forward-thinking music that moves freely between experimental traditions and popular forms.     ['NNA Tapes', 'nnatapes.music', 'brand/marks/nna-tapes.svg', 1, 'Experimental / Avant-Pop',

  // Names, technical categories and descriptions supplied by the client, verbatim.
  DEFAULT_GRP_SERVICES = [
    ['PR', 'Music Publicity',
      'Relationship-driven music publicity built around the story and audience of each release.       'Warehousing and direct-to-fan fulfillment built for music, with inventory management, pick-and-pack, Bandcamp and webstore order fulfillment, shipping, inventory synchronization across sales channels, and bulk or tour drop-shipping for labels and artists.'],

  ];

  // Three products under Haulix plus custom work. Project management and the
  // client portal are parts of Release Manager, not products of their own.
  // Descriptions are drafted from the brief and are placeholders for real copy.
  // Names, technical categories and descriptions supplied by the client, verbatim.
  DEFAULT_GRP_PRODUCTS = [
    ['Haulix Promo', 'Established', 'Music Promotion & Pitching Platform', 'try.haulix.com',
      'Haulix Promo is an end-to-end pitching and promo platform for labels, artists, and publicists.     ['Haulix Media Connect', 'Beta', 'Music Media API', '',
      'A continuously refreshed global music-media database and headless API, built to map the people, companies, outlets, and radio organizations covering music, with integrated tools for media discovery, press and radio monitoring, coverage tracking, and reporting.'],
    ['Haulix Release Manager', 'Beta', 'Music Project Management Platform', 'releasemanager.net',
      'A purpose-built project management system for music releases, bringing together budgets, timelines, tasks, client portals, assets, approvals, and release workflows.'],
    ['Haulix Commerce Connect', 'Beta', 'Music Commerce & Fulfillment Platform', '',
      'A music-centric commerce and fulfillment system connecting storefronts, orders, SKU-level inventory, warehouse operations, and shipping across Bandcamp, Shopify, and other sales channels.']
