          borderBottom: '1px solid var(--hair)',
          background: this.rowBg(active), boxShadow: this.rowBar(active),
          fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace", fontSize: '12px'
        },
        swatch: { width: '10px', height: '10px', borderRadius: '50%', background: 'var(--gold)', opacity: active ? 0.9 : 0.4 },
        name: name,
        nameStyle: { color: 'var(--ink)', overflow: 'hidden', textOverflow: 'ellipsis', whiteSpace: 'nowrap' },
        count: counts[name] === 1 ? '1 record' : counts[name] + ' records',
        countStyle: { fontSize: '9px', letterSpacing: '0.12em', textTransform: 'uppercase', color: 'var(--ink55)' },
        // Third instance of the same defect, fixed with the other two: styled,
        // cursor'd on the row, and no handler. Artist rows get the artistTile
        // menu — play top tracks, add, into the records — not the track one.
        dots: this.openCtx('artistTile', null),
        // Same two corrections as the release row above: --ink40 at rest is 2.67:1
        // in light, and 20px of width is half the target this file requires.
        dotsStyle: { display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', width: '40px', height: '40px', margin: '0 -10px', fontSize: '15px', cursor: 'pointer', color: active ? 'var(--gold)' : 'var(--ink70)' },
        enter: () => this.hoverEnter({ hoverRow: 'a' + i }),
        leave: () => this.setState({ hoverRow: null }),
        // ⚠ WAS `() => {}` (2026-09-09, code audit 2c) — a first-class surface
        // painting a hover ground, a gold bar and cursor:pointer over a handler
        // that did nothing. MAP_VIEW_FOR sends both `artists` and `artist` here.
        // The row opens the artist's page, which is what the same row does in
        // every other list; the ⋮ beside it keeps the artistTile menu.
        // On the map the row stays on the map — it fills the column with that
        // artist's records, the same shape a browse pick takes. Off the map it
        // opens the artist's page. Same rule as goArtistsFn: the surface you are
        // on is the surface you stay on.
        // ⚠ THIS SITE HAD THE RULE FIRST AND TESTED ONLY 'star', so ATH's own
        // map escaped it — the one label whose landing IS a map. Through
        // artistPatch now, which tests both; behaviour on 'star' is unchanged,
        // just computed in one place.
        click: () => this.setState(this.artistPatch(name))
      };
    });

    const bc = st.browseCat == null ? 3 : st.browseCat;
    const cat = this.catRows()[bc];
    const filterMode = st.browseMode === 'filter';
    const picks = st.picks || [];
    const browseCats = this.catRows().map((c, i) => ({
      key: 'bc' + i,
      box: {
        // 14d: 52px rows on a phone, with a hairline under each — the desktop
        // rail's 38px is below the touch-target rule.
        display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: (narrow ? '12px' : '10px'),
        height: (narrow ? '52px' : '38px'), padding: narrow ? 0 : '0 9px',
        flexShrink: 0, boxSizing: 'border-box',
        borderRadius: narrow ? 0 : '4px',
        borderBottom: narrow ? '1px solid var(--hair)' : 'none',
        cursor: 'pointer',
        background: (narrow || i !== bc) ? 'transparent' : 'var(--tile)',
        fontSize: (narrow ? '15px' : '13px'),
        color: (!narrow && i === bc) ? 'var(--gold)' : 'var(--ink)'
      },
      dot: { width: (narrow ? '9px' : '7px'), height: (narrow ? '9px' : '7px'), flexShrink: 0, borderRadius: '50%', background: c[3], boxShadow: narrow ? '0 0 0 1px var(--hair20)' : 'none' },
      name: c[0], count: c[1],
      countStyle: { fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace", fontSize: '9px', color: i === bc ? 'var(--gold)' : 'var(--ink40)' },
      enter: () => this.hoverEnter({ browseCat: i, browseStep: 'values' })
    }));
    const browseItems = cat[2].map((n, i) => {
      const ph = /pending/i.test(n);
      const keyId = cat[0] + ':' + n;
      const checked = picks.indexOf(keyId) > -1;
      const on = filterMode ? checked : st.browseSel === keyId;
      const hov = st.browseHover === bc + ':' + i;
      return {
        key: 'bi' + i,
        box: {
          display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '10px', height: '40px', flexShrink: 0,
          padding: '0 4px', boxSizing: 'border-box', cursor: ph ? 'default' : 'pointer',
          borderBottom: '1px solid var(--hair)',
          background: hov && !ph ? 'rgba(219,191,61,0.07)' : 'transparent'
        },
        mark: filterMode ? (checked ? '✓' : '') : '',
        markStyle: filterMode ? {
          width: '13px', height: '13px', flexShrink: 0, borderRadius: '3px',
          border: '1px solid ' + (checked ? 'var(--gold)' : 'var(--hair20)'),
          background: checked ? 'var(--gold)' : 'transparent',
          display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', fontSize: '9px', color: 'var(--ongold)'
        } : { display: 'none' },
        name: n,
        nameStyle: { flex: 1, minWidth: 0, overflow: 'hidden', textOverflow: 'ellipsis', whiteSpace: 'nowrap', fontSize: '13px', fontStyle: ph ? 'italic' : 'normal', color: ph ? 'var(--ink40)' : on ? 'var(--gold)' : 'var(--ink70)' },
        arrow: filterMode ? '' : '→',
        arrowStyle: filterMode ? { display: 'none' } : { fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace", fontSize: '10px', color: on || hov ? 'var(--gold)' : 'var(--ink40)' },
        enter: () => this.hoverEnter({ browseHover: bc + ':' + i }),
        leave: () => this.setState({ browseHover: null }),
        click: () => {
          if (ph) return;
          if (filterMode) {
            this.setState({ picks: checked ? picks.filter(p => p !== keyId) : picks.concat([keyId]) });
          } else {
            // RESULTS COME BACK IN THE MODE YOU SEARCHED FROM (2026-09-01).
            // SUPERSEDES "GRID FIRST", which sent every browse pick to the
            // catalogue grid no matter where it was used. That threw a visitor
            // out of the star field for asking it a question — and the star
            // field is the one place where a facet is worth SEEING, because the
            // answer is a shape rather than a list.
            //
            // So: browse from the map, get the map, filtered. Browse from the
            // grid, get the grid, filtered. Either way `view: 'results'` and the
            // facet chip carry the set, so Grid|Map still swaps between two
            // views of the SAME answer rather than resetting it.
            this.setState({
              route: isStar ? 'star' : 'catalogue',
              view: 'results', browseSel: keyId, resultLabel: n, resultCat: cat[0],
              open: true, lock: null, hoverStar: null, browseOpen: false
            });
          }
        }
      };
    });

    const modeBtn = (on) => ({
      flex: 1, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', height: '40px',
      boxSizing: 'border-box', borderRadius: '5px', cursor: 'pointer',
      background: on ? 'var(--tile)' : 'transparent',
      border: '1px solid ' + (on ? 'var(--hair20)' : 'var(--hair)'),
      color: on ? 'var(--gold)' : 'var(--ink55)',
      fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace", fontSize: '10px', letterSpacing: '0.14em', textTransform: 'uppercase', whiteSpace: 'nowrap'
    });

    const chips = picks.map(p => {
      const parts = p.split(':');
      const c = this.catRows().find(x => x[0] === parts[0]);
      return {
        key: p,
        box: { display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '6px', height: '28px', padding: '0 9px', boxSizing: 'border-box', borderRadius: '4px', background: 'rgba(219,191,61,0.16)', border: '1px solid var(--goldsoft)', fontSize: '11px', color: 'var(--gold)', whiteSpace: 'nowrap', cursor: 'pointer' },
        dot: { width: '7px', height: '7px', borderRadius: '50%', background: c ? c[3] : 'var(--gold)' },
        label: parts[1],
        remove: () => this.setState({ picks: picks.filter(x => x !== p) })
      };
    });

    // ── THE COLUMN'S TOUR VIEW READS THE REAL DATES (2026-09-09) ─────────
    // ⚠ THIS WAS FOUR INVENTED ROWS. [0,1,2,3] mapped to 'Artist pending',
    // 'City pending', 'Venue pending' and a '—' where the date goes — a
    // placeholder glyph in a data slot and three fabricated rows, on a surface
    // MAP_VIEW_FOR now sends every visitor to from the Tour page. It touched no
    // table at all while every other "On tour" block in the file resolves
    // through TOUR.
    //
    // Same source as the tour page: TOUR is [artist, date, venue, city, badge,
    // ticketUrl]. No dates → no rows, and the column renders nothing rather
    // than a picture of a tour.
    // TOUR when it is supplied; the two month tables otherwise — the same
    // fallback ladder the tour page walks, so the column and the page can never
    // list different dates. [day, dow, artist, 'venue · city', note] is the month
    // tables' shape; the injected table is mapped into it above.
    const tourRows = (() => {
      const T = this.data('TOUR') || [];
      if (T.length) return T.map(r => ({ date: r[1] || '', artist: r[0] || '', venue: r[2] || '', city: r[3] || '', href: r[5] || '' }));
      const month = (rows, m) => (rows || []).map(r => {
        const where = String(r[3] || '').split(' \u00b7 ');
        return { date: m + ' ' + r[0], artist: r[2] || '', venue: where[0] || '', city: where[1] || '', href: '' };
      });
      return month(this.data('TOUR_APRIL'), 'Apr').concat(month(this.data('TOUR_MAY'), 'May'));
    })();
    const tour = tourRows.slice(0, 12).map((r, i) => ({
      key: 't' + i,
      box: {
        display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '92px 1fr auto', gap: '12px', alignItems: 'center',
        padding: '12px 22px', boxSizing: 'border-box', borderBottom: '1px dotted var(--hair)',
        fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace", fontSize: '11px'
      },
      date: r.date,
      dateStyle: { color: 'var(--gold)', opacity: 0.7, fontVariantNumeric: 'tabular-nums' },
      artist: r.artist,
      artistStyle: { fontSize: '11px', fontWeight: 600, textTransform: 'uppercase', color: 'var(--ink)' },
      city: r.city,
      cityStyle: { color: 'var(--ink70)' },
      venue: r.venue,
      venueStyle: { fontSize: '10px', color: 'var(--ink55)' },
      // The chip is a real link where the date has one, and absent where it does
      // not — the same rule the tour page's own Tickets chip now follows.
      ticket: r.href ? 'Tickets' : '',
      ticketHref: r.href || null,
      ticketStyle: r.href
        ? { display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', height: '40px', padding: '0 10px', boxSizing: 'border-box', borderRadius: '4px', border: '1px solid var(--hair20)', color: 'var(--ink70)', cursor: 'pointer' }
        : { display: 'none' }
    }));

    // ── THE COLUMN AND THE ALBUM PAGE SHARE ONE TRACKLIST (2026-09-04) ────
    // This read TRACKS — a flat table with no record key — so the column
    // printed "Passing Fires" as track 01 of whatever record was locked while
    // the album page printed the right list for the same record: two surfaces,
    // one record, two answers. Both call recTrackRows(pair) now: REC_TRACKS
    // narrowed to this record on the site, the mock in the tool. TRACKS is
    // retired as a page source (designer request 2).
    const TR_ALL = this.recTrackRows(pair);
    const TR = st.allTracks ? TR_ALL : TR_ALL.slice(0, 5);
    // ── THE DRAWER'S TRACK ROWS PLAY (ported from V1 r55, 2026-09-09 audit) ──
    // ⚠ THE V1→V2 PORT MISSED THIS and the audit found it: V2 sti
